#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,PartialEq,Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all="kebab-case")]
pub enum Reachability { AuthoredFixtureTrace, TrustedSeedOnly, SyntheticOperation }
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Counterexample {pub reachability:Reachability,pub invariant_site:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub viewer:String,pub operation:String,pub effect:String,pub row:Value,pub old:Option<Value>,pub supporting_rows:BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,pub rule_path:String,pub scope:String, pub observations:Option<paired::Observations>,pub history:Vec<histories::Step>}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Default,Serialize)]
pub struct Campaign {pub operations:BTreeMap<String,usize>,pub sequences:usize,pub attempted:usize,pub admitted:usize,pub refused:usize,pub violations:Vec<Counterexample>,pub boundary_failures:Vec<String>,pub unsupported:BTreeSet<String>}
fn id(table:&str)->Value {Value::Id(format!("security-{table}"))}
fn sample(kind:&C,variant:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match kind{
    C::Optional(inner)=>if variant&4!=0{sample(inner,variant).unwrap_or(Value::Null)}else{Value::Null},C::Id|C::Principal=>Value::Id(if variant&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()),
    C::Ref{table}=>id(table),C::Text{max}=>Value::String("x".repeat((*max).min(20) as usize)),
    C::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(variant%variants.len())?.clone()),
    C::Int|C::Decimal{..}|C::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),C::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),
    C::Bool=>Value::Bool((variant>>2)&1==0),C::Time=>Value::Int(NOW-1000),C::Json{..}=>Value::Null,C::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),
    C::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),C::ListRange{of,min,..}=>Value::Array((0..*min).map(|_|sample(of,variant)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
    C::Image{..}=>Value::String("$security:image".into()),C::File{..}=>Value::String("$security:file".into()),C::Video{..}=>return None,
})}
fn needed(schema:&Schema,name:&str,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){
    if !out.insert(name.into()){return;}let Some(t)=schema.table(name)else{return};
    for k in t.columns.values(){if let C::Ref{table}=k.required(){needed(schema,table,out);}}
    for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{for dep in kernel::dependencies(r){needed(schema,&dep,out);}}
}
fn snapshot(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,false)
}
fn snapshot_mode(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize,timed:bool)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    let mut names=BTreeSet::new();needed(&bound.schema,&inv.table,&mut names);
    if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy{for dep in kernel::dependencies(policy){needed(&bound.schema,&dep,&mut names);}}
    if let Some(mut syntax)=inv.syntax.clone(){super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut syntax,&mut |e| {
        if let ast::Expr::Read(r)|ast::Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=e {needed(&bound.schema,&r.table,&mut names);}
    });}
    if let Some(op)=&inv.operation && let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==op){for kind in p.program.args.values(){if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind{needed(&bound.schema,table,&mut names);}}}
    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();
    let mut enum_case=variant;
    for name in names {
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&name];
        if bound.schema.maintain_for(&name).is_some(){continue;}
        let mut fields=BTreeMap::new();fields.insert("id".into(),id(&name));
        let mut principal_index=0usize;
        for (n,k) in &t.columns{
            if n=="id"||t.engine_field(n)||t.maintained.contains_key(n){continue;}
            let sample_variant=if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){let n=enum_case % variants.len();enum_case/=variants.len();n}else if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal){let n=((variant>>(3+principal_index.min(5)))&1)|(variant&4);principal_index+=1;n}else{variant};
            let value=sample(k,sample_variant)
                .ok_or_else(||format!("{name}.{n}: asset ledger/required sample is not available"))?;
            fields.insert(n.clone(),value);
        }
        if timed || t.read_lifetime_sensitive() {for (n,k) in &t.columns {if matches!(k.required(),C::Time)&&(!k.is_optional()||variant&4!=0) {
            fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_ref()==Some(n){2000}else{-1000}));
        }}}
        for constraint in &t.constraints{if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=constraint
            && matches!(fields.get(field),Some(Value::String(_))) {fields.insert(field.clone(),Value::String("x".repeat((*min).max(1) as usize)));}}
        // Named primary keys used by role probes must select the challenged
        // viewer; changing the enum represents a current-role change while
        // derived assignment/membership rows remain present.
        if name!=inv.table {for (i,(n,k)) in t.columns.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k,C::Principal)).enumerate(){fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Id(if variant & (1 << (i+3)) == 0 {VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into())); let _=k;}}
        out.insert(name,vec![Value::Row(fields.into())]);
    }
    Ok(out)
}
struct Snapshot<'a>{schema:&'a Schema,rows:&'a BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>}
impl Probe for Snapshot<'_>{
    fn exists(&mut self,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[Value],equalities:&[(String,Value)])->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{
        let t=&self.schema.tables[table];let Some(idx)=t.index(index)else{return Ok(false)};
        if idx.components.len()!=key.len(){return Ok(false);}
        if idx.unique&&idx.components.iter().any(|c|c.column().is_some_and(|n|t.columns.get(n).is_some_and(C::is_optional)))&&key.contains(&Value::Null){return Ok(false);}
        Ok(self.rows.get(table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|live(t,r)).any(|r|idx.components.iter().zip(key).all(|(c,v)|c.column().is_some_and(|n|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v)))&&equalities.iter().all(|(n,v)|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v))))
    }
    fn created(&mut self,_:&str,_:&str,_:&[Value])->bool{false}
    // Policies describe raw independent intent. The observer itself must still
    // execute through the engine's live/authorized reference implementation.
    fn reference(&mut self,table:&str,id:&Value,field:&str)->Result<Option<Value>,RuntimeError>{
        Ok(self.rows.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(id)&&live(&self.schema.tables[table],r)).and_then(|r|r.field(field).ok()).cloned())
    }
}
fn live(t:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,row:&Value)->bool{
    !t.expired(row,NOW)&&t.not_before.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|match row.field(c){Ok(Value::Int(n))=>*n<=NOW,Ok(Value::Null)=>true,_=>false})
}
fn policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value)->Result<bool,String>{
    
    let Some(p)=inv.policy.as_ref() else{return inv.syntax.as_ref().and_then(|e|super::concrete::evaluate(e,row,rows,&bound.schema,viewer)).ok_or_else(||"concrete policy expression is unknown/unsupported (including clock and typed literal coercions)".into());};
    
    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    rules::evaluate(p,viewer,old,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows}).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn program(name:&str,kind:ProgramKind,body:Vec<Stmt>)->Program{Program{name:name.into(),kind,args:BTreeMap::new(),body,read_time_granularity_ms:None,returned_shapes:BTreeMap::new(),native:false}}
fn engine(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probe:Program)->Result<Engine,String>{
    engine_with(bound,rows,vec![probe])
}
fn engine_with(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
    let store=Store::memory().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let mut programs=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();programs.extend(probes);
    let mut engine=Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),programs,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    fn hydrate(engine:&mut Engine,kind:&C,value:&Value)->Result<Value,String>{
        match (kind,value) {
            (C::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>hydrate(engine,inner,v),
            (C::List{of,..}|C::ListRange{of,..},Value::Array(xs))=>xs.iter().map(|v|hydrate(engine,of,v)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map(Value::Array),
            (C::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>engine.store_asset(VIEWER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|format!("verified image fixture: {e}")),
            (C::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(engine,VIEWER,types),
            _=>Ok(value.clone()),
        }
    }
    let mut seeded=rows.iter().filter(|(name,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(name).is_none()).map(|(name,rs)|{
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];
        let clean=rs.iter().map(|r|if let Value::Row(fields)=r {Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect())}else{r.clone()}).collect();
        (name.clone(),clean)
    }).collect::<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>();
    for (table,values) in &mut seeded {for row in values {for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns {
        if let Ok(v)=row.field(column) {
            let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,v)?;
            if let Value::Row(fields)=row {fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}
        }
    }}}
    engine.seed_at(seeded.iter().flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>(),NOW).map_err(|e|format!("generated snapshot rejected by schema: {e}"))?;
    let actual=readback(&engine)?;
    for (name,values) in &actual {for row in values {
        snapback4_core::engine::validate::validate_row(&bound.schema,name,row).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        for c in &bound.schema.tables[name].constraints {if let Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table,index,key}=c {
            let rule=RuleExpr::Exists{table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),key:key.clone(),provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed,equalities:Vec::new()};
            if !rules::evaluate(&rule,VIEWER,None,row,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows:&actual}).map_err(|e|e.to_string())? {return Err(format!("{name}: trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"));}
        }}
    }}
    Ok(engine)
}
fn replay(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,word:&str,row:&Value)->Result<(bool,Value),String>{
    let name="$security.probe";let table=inv.table.clone();let key=id(&table);
    let read=Read{site:"security challenge".into(),table:table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),key:vec![Expr::Literal(key.clone())],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed};
    let mut seeds=rows.clone();
    let probe=match word{
        "read"=>program(name,ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(read))]),
        "insert"=>{seeds.remove(&table);program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Insert{table,row:Expr::Literal(row.clone())},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])},
        "delete"=>program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Delete{table,key:Expr::Literal(key)},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]),
        "update"=>{
            let definition=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
            let changed=definition.columns.iter().filter(|(n,_)|n.as_str()!="id"&&!definition.engine_field(n))
                .filter(|(n,_)|inv.column.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|c==*n))
                .filter(|(n,_)|!definition.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(n))))
                .find_map(|(n,k)| {let old=row.field(n).ok()?;let next=match (k.required(),old){
                    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
                    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|v|Some(Value::String((*v).clone()))!=Some(old.clone()))?.clone()),
                    (C::Text{max},Value::String(v)) if v.chars().count()<*max as usize=>Value::String(format!("{v}x")),
                    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(v))=>Value::Int(v+1),_=>return None};
                    Some((n.clone(),next))});
            let Some((column,value))=changed else{return Err("no valid changed mutable-column sample".into());};
            program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Update{table,key:Expr::Literal(key),patch:Expr::Literal(Value::Map([(column,value)].into_iter().collect()))},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])
        },_=>return Err("row integrity/transition challenge is not an operation admission test".into()),
    };
    let mut engine=engine(bound,&seeds,probe)?;
    if word=="read"{return engine.query(name,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map(|q|(q.data!=Value::Null,row.clone())).map_err(|e|e.to_string());}
    let outcome=engine.mutate(name,None,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    if !matches!(outcome.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..}){return Ok((false,row.clone()));}
    let next=engine.store().rows(&inv.table).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.into_iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
    if word=="delete"{return if next.is_none(){Ok((true,row.clone()))}else{Err("delete returned sent without removing the target".into())};}
    let next=next.ok_or("sent write has no committed target image")?;
    if word=="update" {
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
        let changed=table.columns.keys().filter(|c|!table.engine_field(c)&&!table.maintained.contains_key(*c))
            .any(|c|row.field(c).ok()!=next.field(c).ok()&&inv.column.as_ref().is_none_or(|column|column==c));
        if !changed{return Err("sent no-op/implicit-only update has no changed declared target column".into());}
    }
    Ok((true,next))
}
fn readback(engine:&Engine)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{engine.schema().tables.keys().map(|t|engine.store().rows(t).map(|rs|(t.clone(),rs)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).collect()}

fn operation_preflight(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<(),String>{
    let Some(name)=&inv.operation else{return Ok(());};
    let p=&bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==name).ok_or("operation did not bind")?.program;
    if !super::census::body(&p.body){return Err(format!("{name}: opaque/loop/scan path requires write-boundary tracing"));}
    let rule=inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("operation policy outside rule fragment")?;
    if rule.any_term(&|t|!matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))){return Err("operation policy requires write-boundary row-image tracing".into());}
    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
    if writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).count()>1{return Err(format!("{name}: multiple target writes need executed-effect tracing; net images do not identify delete/reinsert"));}
    let unstable=kernel::dependencies(rule).iter().any(|dep|{
        let hits=writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==dep).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        !hits.is_empty()&&!(dep==&inv.table&&hits.len()==1&&hits[0].1=="insert")
    });
    if unstable||!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty()||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.maintained.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())){
        return Err("operation policy dependency may change before/after its write boundary; snapshot correspondence unproved".into());
    }Ok(())
}

/// Schema-only engine challenges. Successful synthetic operations refute the
/// declared table-authority contract, not application-operation reachability.
pub fn run(bound:&Bound)->Campaign{run_selected(bound,None)}
/// `check` searches unproved direct obligations; unsearched blockers remain
/// explicitly unproved. Default `test` passes None and runs the whole set.
pub fn run_selected(bound:&Bound,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>)->Campaign{
    let mut out=Campaign::default();
    // A direct component theorem needs no repeated concrete search for that
    // same component. Indirect observers, temporal reads and actual authored
    // operations below still run; the whole declaration remains unproved.
    let discharged=super::analyze(bound).invariants.into_iter()
        .filter(|v|v.direct_rule==Status::Proved&&v.invariant.operation.is_none()
            && (matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"|"insert"|"delete")
                || matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"update"|"write")&&bound.schema.tables[&v.invariant.table].written_by.is_empty()))
        .map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut assertions=bound.invariants.clone();
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.is_some(){continue;}
        if inv.operation.is_some()||matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"){continue;}
        for p in &bound.programs {if p.program.kind!=ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}
            let relevant=ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(table,kind)|table==&inv.table&&(inv.target=="write"||inv.target==*kind||*kind=="upsert"));
            if relevant{let mut op=inv.clone();op.operation=Some(p.program.name.clone());assertions.push(op);}
        }
    }
    for inv in &assertions{
        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.is_some(){continue;}
        if sites.is_some_and(|sites|!sites.contains(&inv.site.to_string())){continue;}
        if inv.operation.is_none()&&discharged.contains(&inv.site.to_string()){continue;}
        if let Err(why)=operation_preflight(bound,inv){out.unsupported.insert(why);continue;}
        let mut found=false;
        let combinations=bound.schema.tables.values().flat_map(|t|t.columns.values()).filter_map(|k|if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){Some(variants.len())}else{None}).fold(1usize,usize::saturating_mul).max(24);
        if combinations>512{out.unsupported.insert("schema enum Cartesian matrix exceeds 512 samples; remaining tuples are not covered".into());}
        for sample in 0..=combinations.min(512) {
            let variant=sample.saturating_sub(1);
            let mut rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
            // Try explicit policy field literals before the unchanged enum
            // matrix. A value such as status='cancelled' need not wait behind
            // hundreds of unrelated enum tuples. This is only a candidate;
            // ordinary validation and engine replay still decide refutation.
            if sample==0 {
                fn pin(rule:&RuleExpr,row:&mut Value)->bool{match rule{
                    RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(v))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(v))|
                    RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(v),RuleTerm::Field(f))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Literal(v),RuleTerm::Field(f))=>{
                        if let Value::Row(fields)=row&&fields.contains_key(f){fields.insert(f.clone(),v.clone());true}else{false}
                    },
                    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().fold(false,|any,r|pin(r,row)||any),
                    RuleExpr::Not(r)=>pin(r,row),_=>false,
                }}
                if !inv.policy.as_ref().zip(rows.get_mut(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first_mut())).is_some_and(|(p,row)|pin(p,row)){continue;}
            }
            let mut seeded=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(Read{site:"security read".into(),table:inv.table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),key:vec![Expr::Literal(id(&inv.table))],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed}))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
            let rows=match readback(&seeded){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
            let Some(row)=rows.get(&inv.table).and_then(|r|r.first())else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no generated ordinary row",inv.table));break;};
            for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER,"account:security-no-role","service:security"]{
                if inv.target=="always" {
                    match policy(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,None,row){
                        Ok(false)=>{out.attempted+=1;out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::TrustedSeedOnly,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:"$security.seed_at".into(),effect:"always".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:rows.clone(),rule_path:"trusted seed ingress; all typed and final-exists constraints rechecked".into(),scope:"actual seed_at commit/read-back; ordinary application reachability is not claimed".into()});found=true;},
                        Ok(true)=>{out.attempted+=1;},Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);}
                    }if found{break;}continue;
                }
                if let Some(operation)=&inv.operation {
                    let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==operation)else{continue;};
                    let permitted=policy(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,None,row);
                    let Some(args)=arguments(&p.program.args,variant)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{operation}: arguments need fixture assets or unsupported samples"));continue;};
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    let mut seeded=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                    match seeded.mutate(operation,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)) {
                        Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})=>{
                            let current=readback(&seeded).unwrap_or_default();
                            let changed=current.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|!rows.get(&inv.table).is_some_and(|old|old.contains(r))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                            for next in changed {
                                let old=rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|old|old.field("id").ok()==next.field("id").ok());
                                // An upsert must be attributed to its executed arm;
                                // a column contract cannot be refuted by editing a sibling.
                                if inv.target=="update"&&old.is_none() || inv.target=="insert"&&old.is_some() || inv.target=="delete" {continue;}
                                if let Some(column)=&inv.column && old.is_some_and(|old|old.field(column).ok()==next.field(column).ok()){continue;}
                                if permitted==Ok(false){out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:operation.clone(),effect:if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"}.into(),row:next.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:rows.clone(),rule_path:format!("authored operation {operation}"),scope:"actual authored mutation committed a changed image from a schema-valid seeded snapshot".into()});found=true;break;}
                            }
                        },_=>out.refused+=1,
                    }
                    if found{break;}continue;
                }
                for word in super::effects(inv){
                    let mut admission_rows=rows.clone();if word=="insert"{admission_rows.remove(&inv.table);}
                    let allowed=match policy(bound,inv,&admission_rows,viewer,(word!="insert").then_some(row),row){Ok(v)=>v,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                    if allowed{continue;}out.attempted+=1;
                    let replayed=if word=="read" {seeded.query("$security.seed",Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map(|q|(q.data!=Value::Null,row.clone())).map_err(|e|e.to_string())}else{replay(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,word,row)};
                    match replayed{
                        Ok((false,_))=>out.refused+=1,
                        Ok((true,next))=>{
                            let mut args=BTreeMap::new();
                            if word=="update"{let changes=bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.keys().filter(|c|row.field(c).ok()!=next.field(c).ok()).filter_map(|c|next.field(c).ok().map(|v|(c.clone(),v.clone()))).collect();args.insert("$syntheticPatch".into(),Value::Map(changes));}
                            out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args,viewer:viewer.into(),operation:format!("$security.{}.{}",inv.table,word),effect:word.into(),row:next,old:(word=="update"||word=="delete").then(||row.clone()),supporting_rows:admission_rows.clone(),rule_path:bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].rules.for_effect(super::effect(word)).quarry(),scope:if word=="update"{"schema-valid seeded snapshot; old-image policy false at an actual changed update; committed next image and patch shown; application reachability is not claimed".into()}else{"schema-valid seeded snapshot; synthetic engine table operation; application reachability is not claimed".into()}});found=true;break;},
                        Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: {e}",inv.table,word));},
                    }
                }
                if found{break;}
            }
            if found{break;}
        }
    }
    column_reads::generated(bound,&mut out,sites);
    paired::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
    timed::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
    out.unsupported.insert("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require a live server; engine principals are authenticated contexts".into());
    out.unsupported.insert("offline/revoked-device access and retained history require device boundary qualification".into());out
}
pub fn apply(report:&mut Report,campaign:&Campaign){report.campaign_coverage.extend(campaign.unsupported.iter().cloned());for c in &campaign.violations{for v in &mut report.invariants{if v.invariant.site.to_string()==c.invariant_site{
    if v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|old.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace)&&c.reachability!=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace{continue;}
    v.status=Status::Refuted;v.counterexample=Some(c.clone());v.reasons.push("concrete policy-false snapshot admitted by the actual engine; finite test evidence, not a reachability theorem".into());
}}}}

fn arguments(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,variant:usize)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    use ir::ArgType as A;
    fn one(k:&A,v:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match k{
        A::Ref{table}=>id(table),A::Principal=>Value::Id(if v&1==0{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),A::Id=>Value::Id("security-argument".into()),A::Cursor|A::Optional(_)=>Value::Null,
        A::Text{min,max,..}=>Value::String("z".repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize)),A::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(v%variants.len())?.clone()),A::Int|A::Decimal{..}|A::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),A::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),A::Time=>Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000),A::Bool=>Value::Bool(v&2==0),A::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),A::Json{..}=>Value::Null,A::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),A::Image{..}|A::File{..}=>Value::Null,_=>return None,
    })}
    args.iter().map(|(n,k)|one(k,variant).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
}

/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
pub fn operations(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
    assets::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    let fixtures=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    let has_fixture=fixtures.values().any(|r|!r.is_empty());
    if fixtures.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{campaign.unsupported.insert("operation fixture matrix exceeds 1000 rows; provide a focused security fixture".into());return;}
    let mut viewers=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rows) in &fixtures{for row in rows{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!viewers.contains(p){viewers.push(p.clone());}}}}
    for p in &bound.programs{
        if !matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.program.native{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: trusted/system/opaque operation",p.program.name));continue;}
        let target=bound.schema.visible_tables().find(|(t,_)|super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t)).or_else(||bound.schema.visible_tables().next()).map(|(t,_)|t.clone());
        let Some(target)=target else{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no table population for generated arguments",p.program.name));continue;};
        let dummy=Invariant{table:target,target:"read".into(),column:None,predicate:"allow".into(),site:crate::Site::default(),policy:Some(RuleExpr::Allow),operation:Some(p.program.name.clone()),requirements:Vec::new(),syntax:None,unsupported:None};
        let cases=histories::fixture_cases(&p.program.args,&fixtures,&viewers,campaign);
        for (variant,mut args) in cases.into_iter().enumerate(){
            let rows=if has_fixture{fixtures.clone()}else{match snapshot(bound,&dummy,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));break;}}};
            if !has_fixture{for (arg,kind) in &p.program.args {if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind&&let Some(row)=rows.get(table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())&&let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(arg.clone(),id.clone());}}}
            for viewer in &viewers{
                let mut engine=match if has_fixture {fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.matrix",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))}{Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));continue;}};
                campaign.attempted+=1;*campaign.operations.entry(p.program.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
                if p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query{
                    match engine.query(&p.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(_)=>{},Err(e) if e.code=="E_INPUT"=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated arguments rejected as E_INPUT",p.program.name));},Err(_)=>campaign.refused+=1,}
                }else{
                    match engine.mutate(&p.program.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>campaign.refused+=1,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: matrix engine error {e}",p.program.name));},_=>{},}
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn fork(_bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
    if !probes.is_empty(){return Err("fixture probes must be authored operations".into());}
    fixture.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
use super::*;
const LIMIT:usize=512;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}

pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    out.into_iter().collect()
}
fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    }}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn argument_cases(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    fixture_cases(args,&BTreeMap::new(),&[VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into()],out)
}
pub(super) fn fixture_cases(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
        Some(match k{
            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
        })
    }
    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    for (name,k) in args{
        let Some(vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
        let mut next=Vec::new();
        for n in 0..vals.len() {for c in &cases{
            let mut case=c.clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[n%vals.len()].clone());
            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{break;}
        }if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break;}}
        cases=next;
    }cases
}
fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str){
    let committed=|steps:&[Step]|steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&committed(&v.history)>=committed(steps)){return;}
    out.violations.retain(|v|v.invariant_site!=inv.site.to_string()||v.viewer!=viewer||v.reachability!=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace);
    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});
}
fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()){
        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],None),p)==Ok(true));
        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}

        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
            let committed=steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&v.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count()>=committed){continue;}
            if policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            let Ok(id)=row.field("id").and_then(Value::as_str)else{continue;};
            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if policy(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!p.program.native){
                let p=&bp.program;
                let cases=fixture_cases(&p.args,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    out.attempted+=2;
                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
                    if a==b{continue;}
                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){continue;}
                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
                    if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
                    continue 'actors;
                }
            }
        }}
    }
}
fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    for inv in &bound.invariants{
        if inv.target=="read"||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
        if inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some(){
            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
                if policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
            }
        }
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
            if old==Some(row){continue;}
            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
            if inv.target!="always"&&inv.operation.is_none()&&!super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
            if inv.target=="always" {
                if policy(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
            }else{
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table) {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: write policy dependency changes within the operation; tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                if policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old)==Ok(false)){
                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    if bound.invariants.is_empty(){return;}
    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    if initial.values().all(Vec::is_empty){out.unsupported.insert("history needs a trusted fixture; generated snapshots have separate reachability labels".into());return;}
    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
    let probes=Vec::new();
    let mut groups=Vec::new();
    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
        let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program.args,&initial,&actors,out);
        let mut group=Vec::new();
        for args in cases{for viewer in &actors{group.push((&bp.program,viewer.clone(),args.clone()));}}
        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
    }
    let mut candidates=Vec::new();
    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){candidates.push(c.clone());}}}
    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
    // Breadth first, then diagonal pairs: no first step monopolizes the cap.
    let singles=candidates.len().min(LIMIT);
    for n in 0..singles+LIMIT {
        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let k=n-singles;vec![k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()]};
        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
        for i in indices{
            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
                Ok(result)=>{
                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
            }
        }
    }
    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
}

//! A campaign fork retains activated semantics without running adoption again.
use super::*;
impl Engine {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — isolation includes entropy, assets and ephemeral ownership.
    pub fn fork_fixture(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        if self.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.ephemeral.is_some()) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated ephemeral-hub snapshot".into()));
        }
        let store=self.store.fork_memory()?;
        let identity=store.meta("store_id")?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture store has no identity".into()))?;
        Ok(Self {
            timeline_schedule:timeline::Schedule::default(),
            ephemeral:std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(identity,self.generation,&self.schema)),
            store,schema:self.schema.clone(),programs:self.programs.clone(),ceilings:self.ceilings,
            entropy:crate::ids::seed(),minter:crate::ids::Mint::default(),generation:self.generation,
            expiry_after:self.expiry_after.clone(),dev_effects:self.dev_effects,
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native:self.native.clone(),
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native_logs:native::Logs::default(),presence:None,
        })
    }
}
//! Isolated complete fixtures, including captured authority and asset ownership.
use super::*;
impl Store {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — fork committed state, never reinterpret stored rows as new seed input.
    pub fn fork_memory(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        let mut fork = Self::memory()?;
        {
            let backup = rusqlite::backup::Backup::new(&self.conn, &mut fork.conn)?;
            backup.run_to_completion(256, std::time::Duration::ZERO, None)?;
        }
        fork.catalog = self.catalog.clone();
        fork.identity = self.identity.clone();
        fork.server = self.server;
        if self.assets_dir.exists() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&fork.assets_dir).map_err(io)?;
            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&self.assets_dir).map_err(io)? {
                let entry = entry.map_err(io)?;
                if !entry.file_type().map_err(io)?.is_file() {
                    return Err(RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset ledger contains a non-file".into()));
                }
                std::fs::copy(entry.path(), fork.assets_dir.join(entry.file_name())).map_err(io)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(fork)
    }
}

fn io(e:std::io::Error)->RuntimeError {RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string())}

    14	        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}

    16	    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    17	    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    18	    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    19	    out.into_iter().collect()
    20	}
    21	fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    22	    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    23	    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
    24	        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    25	    }}}
    26	    out
    27	}
    28	pub(super) fn argument_cases(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    29	    fixture_cases(args,&BTreeMap::new(),&[VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into()],out)
    30	}
    31	pub(super) fn fixture_cases(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    32	    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
    33	        Some(match k{
    34	            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
    35	            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
    36	            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
    37	            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
    38	            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
    39	            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
    40	        })
    41	    }
    42	    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    43	    for (name,k) in args{
    44	        let Some(vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
    45	        let mut next=Vec::new();
    46	        for n in 0..vals.len() {for c in &cases{
    47	            let mut case=c.clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[n%vals.len()].clone());
    48	            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{break;}
    49	        }if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break;}}
    50	        cases=next;
    51	    }cases
    52	}
    53	fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str){
    54	    let committed=|steps:&[Step]|steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
    55	    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&committed(&v.history)>=committed(steps)){return;}
    56	    out.violations.retain(|v|v.invariant_site!=inv.site.to_string()||v.viewer!=viewer||v.reachability!=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace);
    57	    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});
    58	}
    59	fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    60	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()){
    61	        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],None),p)==Ok(true));
    62	        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
    63	        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}
    64	
    65	        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
    66	            let committed=steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
    67	            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&v.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count()>=committed){continue;}
    68	            if policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row)!=Ok(false){continue;}
    69	            let Ok(id)=row.field("id").and_then(Value::as_str)else{continue;};
    70	            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
    71	            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
    72	            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
    73	            if policy(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row)!=Ok(false){continue;}
    74	            for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!p.program.native){
    75	                let p=&bp.program;
    76	                let cases=fixture_cases(&p.args,rows,actors,out);
    77	                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
    78	                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
    79	                    out.attempted+=2;
    80	                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
    81	                    if a==b{continue;}
    82	                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){continue;}
    83	                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
    84	                    if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
    85	                    continue 'actors;
    86	                }
    87	            }
    88	        }}
    89	    }
    90	}
    91	fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    92	    for inv in &bound.invariants{
    93	        if inv.target=="read"||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
    94	        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
    95	        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    96	        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
    97	        if inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some(){
    98	            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
    99	                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
   100	                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
   101	                if policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
   102	            }
   103	        }
   104	        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
   105	            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
   106	            if old==Some(row){continue;}
   107	            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
   108	            if inv.target!="always"&&inv.operation.is_none()&&!super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
   109	            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
   110	            if inv.target=="always" {
   111	                if policy(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
   112	            }else{
   113	                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
   114	                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table) {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: write policy dependency changes within the operation; tracing required",p.name));continue;}
   115	                if policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old)==Ok(false)){
   116	                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
   117	                }
   118	            }
   119	        }
   120	    }
   121	}
   122	pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   123	    if bound.invariants.is_empty(){return;}
   124	    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   125	    if initial.values().all(Vec::is_empty){out.unsupported.insert("history needs a trusted fixture; generated snapshots have separate reachability labels".into());return;}
   126	    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
   127	    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
   128	    let probes=Vec::new();
   129	    let mut groups=Vec::new();
   130	    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
   131	        let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program.args,&initial,&actors,out);
   132	        let mut group=Vec::new();
   133	        for args in cases{for viewer in &actors{group.push((&bp.program,viewer.clone(),args.clone()));}}
   134	        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
   135	    }
   136	    let mut candidates=Vec::new();
   137	    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){candidates.push(c.clone());}}}
   138	    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   139	    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
   140	    // Breadth first, then diagonal pairs: no first step monopolizes the cap.
   141	    let singles=candidates.len().min(LIMIT);
   142	    for n in 0..singles+LIMIT {
   143	        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let k=n-singles;vec![k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()]};
   144	        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   145	        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
   146	        for i in indices{
   147	            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   148	            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   149	            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
   150	                Ok(result)=>{
   151	                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
   152	                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
   153	                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
   154	                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
   155	                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
   156	                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
   157	            }
   158	        }
   159	    }
   160	    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
   161	}

     4	fn substitute(rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,role:Option<&str>,target:Option<&str>)->RuleExpr{
     5	    let known=|t:&RuleTerm|match t{RuleTerm::Field(c) if c==column=>target.map(|s|snapback4_core::Value::String(s.into())),RuleTerm::Literal(v)=>Some(v.clone()),_=>None};
     6	    match rule{
     7	        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if table==roster&&key.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))&&equalities.iter().any(|(c,_)|c==column)=>{
     8	            let requested=equalities.iter().find(|(c,_)|c==column).and_then(|(_,t)|if let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{Some(s.as_str())}else{None});
     9	            if requested.is_none(){rule.clone()}else if requested==role{RuleExpr::Allow}else{RuleExpr::Deny}
    10	        },
    11	        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(known(a),known(b)){if (a==b)==matches!(rule,RuleExpr::Eq(..)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{RuleExpr::Deny}}else{rule.clone()},
    12	        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
    13	        RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
    14	        RuleExpr::Not(r)=>RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(substitute(r,roster,column,role,target))),
    15	        _=>rule.clone(),
    16	    }
    17	}
    18	pub(super) fn risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,column:&str,targets:&[String],op:ast::WriteOp,requirements:&[RuleExpr])->Option<String>{
    19	    let table=bound.schema.table(roster)?;
    20	    if requirements.iter().any(|r|!r.disjuncts().is_empty()&&r.disjuncts().iter().all(|b|b.service_grant().is_some())){return None;}
    21	    let gates=match op{ast::WriteOp::Insert=>vec![&table.rules.insert],ast::WriteOp::Update=>vec![&table.rules.update],ast::WriteOp::Upsert=>vec![&table.rules.insert,&table.rules.update],ast::WriteOp::Delete=>return None};

    23	        let mut roles=BTreeSet::new();
    24	        fn collect(rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,roles:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
    25	            RuleExpr::Exists{table,equalities,..} if table==roster=>for (c,t) in equalities{if c==column&&let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{roles.insert(s.clone());}},
    26	            RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{collect(r,roster,column,roles)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>collect(r,roster,column,roles),_=>{}
    27	        }}
    28	        for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{collect(r,roster,column,&mut roles);}}roles.into_iter().collect()
    29	    },_=>return None};
    30	    for target in roles.iter().filter(|r|targets.is_empty()||targets.contains(r)){
    31	        for caller in std::iter::once(None).chain(roles.iter().map(|s|Some(s.as_str()))){
    32	            if caller==Some(target.as_str()){continue;}

    34	            if op!=ast::WriteOp::Insert&&let Some(owner)=table.written_by.get(column){branches.push(RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(owner.owner.clone()),RuleTerm::Viewer));}
    35	            let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(RuleExpr::Or(branches)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());

    37	            if !admits{continue;}

    39	                let granted=substitute(rule,roster,column,Some(target),None);let previous=substitute(rule,roster,column,caller,None);
    40	                if kernel::implies(&granted,&previous)==Ok(false){return Some(format!("{} can confer '{target}', which adds {name}.{effect} authority not held by {}",roster,caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}
    41	            }}
    42	        }
    43	    }None
    44	}

    30	pub(super) fn notes(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<Diagnostic>{

    34	        if let RuleExpr::Exists{table:roster,key,equalities,..}=&t.rules.read
    35	            && equalities.is_empty()&&key.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer)) {
    36	            note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by any signed-in user with a matching {roster} row; no particular role or row ownership is required"),"confirm that every roster member should see all these columns; declare intent from the privacy spec and inspect audit");
    37	        }
    38	        for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
    39	            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
    40	            if kind.contains_asset() && (kind.is_optional() || matches!(kind.required(),C::List{..}|C::ListRange{min:0,..})) {
    41	                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE",format!("{name}.{column} permits omitted or empty media evidence"),"if the spec requires evidence, declare a required image/file or [image] 1..N; otherwise retain optionality deliberately (guide security)");
    42	            }
    43	        }
    44	        for leaf in rule_parts(&t.rules.insert) {
    45	            if let RuleExpr::Created {table:parent,..}=leaf
    46	                && bound.schema.table(parent).is_some_and(|p| p.rules.insert.disjuncts().iter().any(|r| matches!(r,RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(_)))))
    47	                && bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other| [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r| rule_parts(r).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if table==name&&matches!(key.as_slice(),[RuleTerm::Viewer])&&equalities.is_empty())))) {
    48	                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_VACUOUS_RULE",format!("{name}: `created {parent}` can bootstrap authority trusted globally by a viewer-only probe; a caller may create their own global administrator grant"),"provision global authority only through an operator/service seat or existing authorized admin; group creation must scope every later grant to that group (guide deploy, first-admin setup)");
    49	            }
    50	        }
    51	        for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)] {
    52	          for branch in rule.disjuncts(){
    53	            if role_test(branch){continue;}
    54	            for dep in kernel::dependencies(branch){if let Some(g)=bound.schema.table(&dep) {
    55	                let rosters=[&g.rules.insert,&g.rules.update].into_iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).filter(|r|mutable_role(bound,r)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    56	                if rosters.iter().any(|r|recipient_role(bound,name,branch,Some(&dep),r,effect))&&(role_test(&g.rules.insert)||role_test(&g.rules.update)){
    57	                    note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: `{}` trusts retained {dep} grants while current role in {} can change",branch.quarry(),rosters.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),"recheck the current role in this branch or revoke every derived grant transactionally on role changes; guide security");
    58	                }
    59	            }}
    60	            if !reader_columns(branch).is_empty()&&[&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update].into_iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).any(|r|mutable_role(bound,&r)&&recipient_role(bound,name,branch,None,&r,effect))&&(role_test(&t.rules.insert)||role_test(&t.rules.update)) {
    61	                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: stored principal `{}` grants authority after a mutable role changes",branch.quarry()),"recheck the current role as well as stored attribution; guide security");
    62	            }
    63	          }
    64	        }
    65	    }
    66	    // Inspect original syntax for argument flow and distinct authored codes.
    67	    // Keeping lints outside Bound.notes preserves the compiler's existing
    68	    // diagnostic contract; check/audit expose this security advice separately.
    69	    let _=exposures;
    70	    notes
    71	}
    72	pub(super) fn source_notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    73	    let mut out=Vec::new();
    74	    let documents=sources.iter().map(|(file,source)|crate::parse_document(file,source).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    75	    let catalog=super::super::catalog::Catalog::build(documents.clone(),&mut Vec::new());
    76	    for doc in documents{
    77	        for p in &doc.programs {
    78	            let mut body=p.prelude.clone();body.extend(p.body.clone());
    79	            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
    80	            let mut guarded=BTreeSet::new();
    81	            for statement in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body) {
    82	                if let Stmt::Require{condition,..}=statement {
    83	                    let mut condition=condition.clone();
    84	                    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut condition,&mut |e|if let Expr::Exists{read,..}=e {
    85	                        for key in &read.keys {if let Expr::Name{name,..}=&key.value {guarded.insert(name.clone());}}
    86	                    });
    87	                }
    88	            }
    89	            super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e|{
    90	                let Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=e else{return};
    91	                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{return};
    92	                let role_columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
    93	                for column in &role_columns {if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column)&&bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other|[&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(table))) {
    94	                    let targets=fields.iter().filter(|f|&f.name==column).filter_map(|f|match &f.value{Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(s),..})=>Some(s.clone()),_=>None}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    95	                    let requirements=catalog.tables.get(table).map(|info|super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,p)).unwrap_or_default();
    96	                    if let Some(risk)=super::privilege::risk(bound,table,column,&targets,*op,&requirements)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),"$privilege".into())) {
    97	                        note(&mut out,site,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION",format!("{}: {risk}; review the grant's delegation policy",p.name),"restrict role assignment to authorized delegation or an operator seat; use guide staging's first-admin recipe (intentional open enrollment may permit this)");
    98	                    }
    99	                }}
   100	                let readers=reader_columns(&t.rules.read);
   101	                for f in fields.iter(){
   102	                    let arg=f.value.as_ref().and_then(|e|if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e{Some(name.as_str())}else{None}).unwrap_or(f.name.as_str());
   103	                    if !guarded.contains(arg)&&readers.contains(&f.name)&&p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==arg)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),f.name.clone())){
   104	                        note(&mut out,site,"N_ARGUMENT_GRANT",format!("{} writes argument {arg} straight into reader-grant column {table}.{}",p.name,f.name),"require the chosen principal's current role and relationship before granting access; caller authority alone does not validate the recipient");
   105	                    }
   106	
   107	                }
   108	            });
   109	            // Distinct absent and denial codes disclose logical existence to
   110	            // callers admitted by a broader table rule, even without payload.
   111	            fn refusals(body:&[Stmt],bound:&Bound,absent:&mut Option<String>,codes:&mut Vec<(Site,String)>){for s in body{match s{
   112	                Stmt::Bind{value:Expr::Assert{value,code,..},..}=>{
   113	                    *absent=None;
   114	                    if let Expr::Read(read)=value.as_ref()&&let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(&read.table){
   115	                        let own=read.keys.iter().any(|k|matches!(&k.value,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"));
   116	                        if !t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable()&&!own {
   117	                            // A table read gate already conflates hidden and absent.
   118	                            // Only broader program-level privacy intent can make
   119	                            // its later refusal distinguish private existence.
   120	                            if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==read.table&&i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&t.rules.read,policy)==Ok(false))){*absent=Some(code.clone());}
   121	                        }
   122	                    }
   123	                },
   124	                Stmt::Require{site,code,..}=>if let Some(a)=absent.as_ref()&&a!=code{codes.push((site.clone(),format!("lookup absence {a}, later denial {code}")));},
   125	                Stmt::If{then_body,else_body,..}=>{refusals(then_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);refusals(else_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);},
   126	                Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>refusals(body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes),_=>{}}}}
   127	            let mut codes=Vec::new();refusals(&p.body,bound,&mut None,&mut codes);
   128	            for (site,why) in codes.into_iter().take(1){note(&mut out,&site,"N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE",format!("{}: {why}; row readership is broader than declared private-existence intent",p.name),"use the same absence/denial code and message for private existence, or tighten the row audience");}
   129	            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Job {
   130	                fn guards(body:&[Stmt],out:&mut Vec<Expr>){for s in body {match s{
   131	                    Stmt::Require{condition,..}=>out.push(condition.clone()),
   132	                    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{out.push(condition.clone());guards(then_body,out);guards(else_body,out);},
   133	                    Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>guards(body,out),_=>{}
   134	                }}}
   135	                let mut job_guards=Vec::new();guards(&p.body,&mut job_guards);
   136	                let clock=job_guards.iter().any(|g|{let mut g=g.clone();let mut found=false;super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut g,&mut |e|if matches!(e,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"){found=true;});found});
   137	                if !clock{continue;}
   138	                let mut writes=BTreeSet::new();let mut b=p.body.clone();
   139	                super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut b,&mut |e|if let Expr::Write{table,fields,..}=e&&fields.iter().any(|f|f.name=="status"){writes.insert(table.clone());});
   140	                for table in writes {for m in doc.programs.iter().filter(|m|m.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation){
   141	                    let mut aliases=BTreeSet::new();
   142	                    for s in &m.body{if let Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=s{
   143	                        let v=if let Expr::Assert{value,..}=value{value.as_ref()}else{value};
   144	                        if matches!(v,Expr::Read(r) if r.table==table){aliases.extend(names.clone());}
   145	                    }}
   146	                    let mut checks=Vec::new();guards(&m.body,&mut checks);let(mut status,mut now)=(false,false);
   147	                    for mut c in checks{super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut c,&mut |e|match e{
   148	                        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"=>now=true,
   149	                        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="status"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Name{name,..} if aliases.contains(name))=>status=true,_=>{}
   150	                    });}
   151	                    let module = std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("schema");
   152	                    let job_name = if module == "schema" { p.name.clone() } else { format!("{module}.{}", p.name) };
   153	                    let already_named = bound.notes.iter().any(|n| n.code == "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" && n.site == m.site
   154	                        && n.message.contains(&format!("column {table}.status)"))
   155	                        && n.message.contains(&format!("scheduled job {job_name} ")));
   156	                    if status&&!now&&!already_named{note(&mut out,&m.site,"N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE",format!("{} gates on {table}.status while clock job {} changes that status; writes can run between the deadline and sweep",m.name,p.name),"enforce the deadline against server now in the mutation too; a sweep cleans up state but does not enforce an exact deadline");}
   157	                }}
   158	            }
   159	        }
   160	    }
   161	    out
   162	}
