//! Narrow stable leading-require reduction for operation-specific authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown write sets never establish stability.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Stmt,ProgramDecl,ProgramKind,InvariantDecl,RuleDecl};
use super::super::catalog::TableInfo;
pub(super) fn resolve<'a>(catalog:&'a Catalog,decl:&InvariantDecl)->Option<(String,&'a ProgramDecl)>{
    if matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"always"|"write"){return None;}
    let matches=catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|{let module=std::path::Path::new(&d.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();d.programs.iter().map(move|p|(super::super::operation_name(&module,&p.name),p))})
        .filter(|(name,p)|p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(name==&decl.target||p.name==decl.target)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if matches.len()==1 {matches.into_iter().next()}else{None}
}
fn safe(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {match rule{
    RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}=>key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().all(safe),
    RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>[a,b].iter().all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Deny|RuleExpr::Public{..}=>true,_=>false,
}}
pub(super) fn requirements(catalog:&Catalog,info:&TableInfo,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<RuleExpr>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body){let Stmt::Require{condition,site,..}=s else{break};
        let decl=RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:condition.clone(),site:site.clone()};
        if let Ok(rule)=super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&decl)&&safe(&rule){out.push(rule);}
    }out
}
pub(super) fn prove(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<bool,String>{
    let p=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|Some(&p.program.name)==inv.operation.as_ref()).ok_or("operation did not bind")?;
    if !safe(inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("unsupported policy")?){return Err("operation policy needs unsupported target-image, negative-probe or live-reference bindings".into());}
    let writes=snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program);
    if !writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table){return Err("operation has no direct write to the named table; implicit effects require a reduction".into());}
    let dependencies=inv.requirements.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|dependencies.contains(t)) {return Err("require dependency can be changed by this operation, including its target write".into());}
    if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.maintained.is_empty()||!t.sequences.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())) || !bound.schema.maintains.is_empty(){return Err("implicit maintain/sequence/referential writes require dependency stability reduction".into());}
    // Source helpers/control flow may hide write sets or viewer changes.
    let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool{match v{serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"For"|"If"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"|"Emit"))||m.values().any(opaque),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false}}
    if opaque(&json){return Err("operation control flow or opaque effects are outside stable leading-require reduction".into());}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(inv.requirements.clone());
    // Table-rule gates can only further restrict a dominating stable require;
    // using the require alone overapproximates effective owner-field gates.
    kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())
}
//! Partial typed row-policy interpreter, never used to establish a proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown/unsupported is not false.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Literal};
use snapback4_core::Value;
type Rows=BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>;
fn boolean(v:Option<Value>)->Option<bool>{match v{Some(Value::Bool(v))=>Some(v),_=>None}}
pub(super) fn evaluate(expr:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<bool>{boolean(value(expr,row,rows,schema,viewer))}
fn value(e:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<Value>{Some(match e{
    Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"=>Value::Id(viewer.into()),
    Expr::Literal{value,..}=>match value{Literal::Str(s)=>Value::String(s.clone()),Literal::Bool(v)=>Value::Bool(*v),Literal::Null=>Value::Null,Literal::Number(s)=>Value::Int(s.parse().ok()?)},
    Expr::Focal{..}=>row.clone(),
    Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..})=>row.field(name).ok()?.clone(),
    Expr::Unary{op,value:x,..} if op=="not"=>Value::Bool(!boolean(value(x,row,rows,schema,viewer))?),
    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..} if op=="and"||op=="or"=>{
        let a=boolean(value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer));let b=boolean(value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer));
        let and=op=="and";let decisive=!and;
        Value::Bool(if a==Some(decisive)||b==Some(decisive){decisive}else if a.is_some()&&b.is_some(){and}else{return None;})
    },
    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=>{
        let a=value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;let b=value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;
        if matches!(op.as_str(),"+"|"-"|"*") {
            let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b))=(a,b)else{return None;};
            let n=match op.as_str(){"+"=>i128::from(a)+i128::from(b),"-"=>i128::from(a)-i128::from(b),_=>i128::from(a)*i128::from(b)};
            return Value::int(n).ok();
        }
        // The binder contextually types principal/id literals. This partial
        // evaluator refuses that coercion rather than using host strings.
        if !matches!((&a,&b),(Value::Id(_),Value::Id(_))|(Value::String(_),Value::String(_))|(Value::Int(_),Value::Int(_))|(Value::Bool(_),Value::Bool(_))|(Value::Null,_)|(_,Value::Null)){return None;}
        Value::Bool(match op.as_str(){"="=>a==b,"!="=>a!=b,"<"|"<="|">"|">="=>if let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b))=(a,b){match op.as_str(){"<"=>a<b,"<="=>a<=b,">"=>a>b,_=>a>=b}}else{return None;},_=>return None})
    },
    Expr::Call{name,args,..} if (name=="count"||name=="len")&&args.len()==1 => {
        let Value::Array(values)=value(&args[0],row,rows,schema,viewer)?else{return None;};
        Value::Int(values.len().try_into().ok()?)
    },
    Expr::Call{name,args,..} if name=="sum"&&args.len()==2 => {
        let Value::Array(values)=value(&args[0],row,rows,schema,viewer)?else{return None;};
        let sum=values.iter().try_fold(0i128,|sum,r|match value(&args[1],r,rows,schema,viewer)? {Value::Int(n)=>sum.checked_add(i128::from(n)),_=>None})?;
        Value::int(sum).ok()?
    },
    Expr::Array{items,..}=>Value::Array(items.iter().map(|e|value(e,row,rows,schema,viewer)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
    Expr::Read(read)|Expr::Exists{read,..}=>{
        let t=schema.table(&read.table)?;
        if t.is_expiring()||t.not_before.is_some()||t.through.is_some()||schema.maintain_for(&read.table).is_some()||read.filter.is_some()||read.range.is_some()||!read.seeks.is_empty()||read.matching.is_some(){return None;}
        let mut pins=Vec::new();
        for key in &read.keys{let column=key.column.as_ref()?;let val=value(&key.value,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;
            let kind=t.columns.get(column)?;
            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
            let typed=match (kind.required(),&val) {
                (_,Value::Null)=>kind.is_optional(),
                (C::Id|C::Principal|C::Ref{..},Value::Id(_))=>true,
                (C::Text{..},Value::String(_))=>true,
                (C::Enum{variants},Value::String(v))=>variants.contains(v),
                (C::Int|C::IntRange{..}|C::Time,Value::Int(_))=>true,
                (C::Bool,Value::Bool(_))=>true,_=>false,
            };
            if !typed{return None;}pins.push((column,val));}
        // Integrity assertions quantify over the complete raw snapshot, not
        // a caller-filtered program population. Temporal/captured populations
        // above remain unsupported. No solver proof uses these samples.
        let selected=rows.get(&read.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|pins.iter().all(|(c,v)|r.field(c).ok()==Some(v))).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if matches!(e,Expr::Exists{..}) {Value::Bool(!selected.is_empty())}else{Value::Array(selected)}
    },
    _=>return None,
})}
//! Paired logical observations, replayed independently in memory.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — two valid worlds and repeatable observations are required.
use super::*;

#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Observations {
    pub present: serde_json::Value,
    pub absent: serde_json::Value,
    pub absent_rows: BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,
}

pub(super) fn observe(engine:&mut Engine,p:&Program,viewer:&str,args:BTreeMap<String,Value>)->Result<serde_json::Value,String>{
    let context=Context::new(viewer,args).at(NOW);
    if p.kind==ProgramKind::Query {
        match engine.query(&p.name,context) {
            Ok(q) if q.unavailable || !q.complete => Err("incomplete/metered query is outside logical payload comparison".into()),
            Ok(q)=>serde_json::to_value(&q.data).map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
            Err(e)=>Err(format!("query error is not an admitted observation: {e}")),
        }
    }else{
        match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,context).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.state {
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{result,..}=>Ok(serde_json::json!({"sent":result})),
            snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{why,current}=>{
                // E_RULE can also mask authorization-budget exhaustion. Only
                // an authored require/constraint carries this rule marker;
                // bound/storage/schema/ambiguous engine refusals cannot be a
                // logical counterexample in our resource-excluding contract.
                if why.rule.is_none()||!matches!(why.family.as_str(),"rule"|"constraint") {
                    return Err(format!("resource/engine or ambiguous mutation refusal {} is outside paired logical comparison",why.code));
                }
                Ok(serde_json::json!({"refusal":{"code":why.code,"message":why.message},"current":current}))
            },
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
        if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observation needs complete bound policy dependencies; unsupported policy AST is not dependency-free",inv.table));continue;}
        if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){continue;}
        // Closed transitive observer set: include maintained targets, inherited
        // readers and every table whose rule probes an affected population.
        // An operation mentioning none of them has no logical input changed
        // by this pair. This prunes experiments, never proves an obligation.
        let mut observers=BTreeSet::from([inv.table.clone()]);
        loop {
            let before=observers.len();
            for m in &bound.schema.maintains {if observers.contains(&m.source){
                observers.insert(m.target.clone());
                if let Some(owner)=&m.private{observers.insert(owner.table.clone());}
            }}
            for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables {
                if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|observers.contains(&p.parent))
                    || [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|d|observers.contains(d))) {
                    observers.insert(name.clone());
                }
            }
            if observers.len()==before{break;}
        }
        // Removing an ordinary row must not manufacture an invalid reference
        // graph. More general paired deletions need a checked closure.
        if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))) {
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired absence needs a referentially valid world closure",inv.table));continue;
        }
        for variant in 0..8 {
            let Ok(present)=snapshot(bound,inv,variant)else{continue;};
            if present.keys().any(|name|bound.schema.tables[name].columns.values().any(C::contains_asset)) {
                out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired fixture asset identities are not coupled",inv.table));continue;
            }
            let Some(row)=present.get(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first()).cloned()else{continue;};
            let mut absent=present.clone();absent.remove(&inv.table);
            // Final-exists validation is a pure judgment of this fixed seeded
            // world, before operation/viewer admission. Retain its rejection
            // across observers, never cache execution/resource/policy results.
            let invalid=std::cell::RefCell::new([None::<String>,None]);
            for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER,"account:security-no-role","service:security"] {
                for bp in &bound.programs {
                    let p=&bp.program;
                    if !matches!(p.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.native {continue;}
                    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
                    let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                    if writes.iter().any(|(table,kind)|table==&inv.table||deps.contains(table)||*kind!="update")
                        || !writes.is_empty() && (!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty() || bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.on_delete.is_empty())) {
                        out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired mutation lacks stable policy/implicit-write and deterministic-id reduction",p.name));continue;
                    }
                    let json=serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();
                    if !observers.iter().any(|table|super::super::audit::mentions(&json,table)){continue;}
                    // Fresh generated ids and opaque viewer/work contexts are
                    // not repeatable functions of these paired inputs.
                    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool {match v{
                        serde_json::Value::String(s)=>s=="NewId"||s=="JobAs",
                        serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"Emit"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"))||m.values().any(opaque),
                        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false,
                    }}
                    if opaque(&json){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque effects excluded from paired determinism",p.name));continue;}
                    let Some(args)=arguments(&p.args,variant)else{continue;};
                    let execute=|seeds:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>|->Result<(serde_json::Value,BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>),String>{
                        let slot=usize::from(std::ptr::eq(seeds,&absent));
                        if let Some(why)=&invalid.borrow()[slot]{return Err(why.clone());}
                        let mut e=match engine(bound,seeds,program("$security.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){
                            Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{
                                if why.ends_with(": trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"){invalid.borrow_mut()[slot]=Some(why.clone());}
                                return Err(why);
                            },
                        };
                        let actual=readback(&e)?;
                        // Judge the hypothetical protected row in each complete
                        // committed world, before any operation can change roles.
                        if policy(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,&row)!=Ok(false){return Err("paired policy is not definitely false in both worlds".into());}
                        observe(&mut e,p,viewer,args.clone()).map(|value|(value,actual))
                    };
                    let ((a,rows),(b,absent_rows))=match (execute(&present),execute(&absent)) {
                        (Ok(a),Ok(b))=>(a,b),
                        (Err(e),_)|(_,Err(e))=>{
                            if !e.starts_with("paired policy is not definitely false") {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.name));}
                            continue;
                        },
                    };
                    out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
                    if a==b {continue;}
                    // The query order is engine-defined. Recreate both worlds
                    // to decline store-scoped cursors or unstable outputs.
                    if !matches!((execute(&present),execute(&absent)),(Ok((aa,_)),Ok((bb,_))) if aa==a&&bb==b){
                        out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired observation is not repeatable",p.name));continue;
                    }
                    out.violations.push(Counterexample {reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:args.clone(),viewer:viewer.into(),
                        operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"read observation".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:rows,
                        rule_path:"paired present/absent engine executions differ while the declared privacy policy is false in both".into(),
                        scope:"schema-valid seed-ingress paired worlds; authenticated logical data/refusal only; no application reachability claim".into(),
                        observations:Some(Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows})});
                    break;
                }
                if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.observations.is_some()){break;}
            }
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.observations.is_some()){break;}
        }
    }
    out.unsupported.insert("paired observations exclude wire sequences/receipts, physical timing and resource/meter differences; HTTP and device histories require separate qualification".into());
}
                for word in super::effects(inv){
                    let mut admission_rows=rows.clone();if word=="insert"{admission_rows.remove(&inv.table);}
                    let allowed=match policy(bound,inv,&admission_rows,viewer,(word!="insert").then_some(row),row){Ok(v)=>v,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                    if allowed{continue;}out.attempted+=1;
                    let replayed=if word=="read" {seeded.query("$security.seed",Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map(|q|(q.data!=Value::Null,row.clone())).map_err(|e|e.to_string())}else{replay(bound,inv,&rows,viewer,word,row)};
                    match replayed{
                        Ok((false,_))=>out.refused+=1,
                        Ok((true,next))=>{
                            let mut args=BTreeMap::new();
                            if word=="update"{let changes=bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.keys().filter(|c|row.field(c).ok()!=next.field(c).ok()).filter_map(|c|next.field(c).ok().map(|v|(c.clone(),v.clone()))).collect();args.insert("$syntheticPatch".into(),Value::Map(changes));}
                            out.admitted+=1;out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args,viewer:viewer.into(),operation:format!("$security.{}.{}",inv.table,word),effect:word.into(),row:next,old:(word=="update"||word=="delete").then(||row.clone()),supporting_rows:admission_rows.clone(),rule_path:bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].rules.for_effect(super::effect(word)).quarry(),scope:if word=="update"{"schema-valid seeded snapshot; old-image policy false at an actual changed update; committed next image and patch shown; application reachability is not claimed".into()}else{"schema-valid seeded snapshot; synthetic engine table operation; application reachability is not claimed".into()}});found=true;break;},
                        Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: {e}",inv.table,word));},
                    }
                }
                if found{break;}
            }
            if found{break;}
        }
    }
    column_reads::generated(bound,&mut out,sites);
    paired::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
    timed::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
    out.unsupported.insert("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require a live server; engine principals are authenticated contexts".into());
    out.unsupported.insert("offline/revoked-device access and retained history require device boundary qualification".into());out
}
pub fn apply(report:&mut Report,campaign:&Campaign){report.campaign_coverage.extend(campaign.unsupported.iter().cloned());for c in &campaign.violations{for v in &mut report.invariants{if v.invariant.site.to_string()==c.invariant_site{
    if v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|old.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace)&&c.reachability!=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace{continue;}
    v.status=Status::Refuted;v.counterexample=Some(c.clone());v.reasons.push("concrete policy-false snapshot admitted by the actual engine; finite test evidence, not a reachability theorem".into());
}}}}

fn arguments(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,variant:usize)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    use ir::ArgType as A;
    fn one(k:&A,v:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match k{
        A::Ref{table}=>id(table),A::Principal=>Value::Id(if v&1==0{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),A::Id=>Value::Id("security-argument".into()),A::Cursor|A::Optional(_)=>Value::Null,
        A::Text{min,max,..}=>Value::String("z".repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize)),A::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(v%variants.len())?.clone()),A::Int|A::Decimal{..}|A::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),A::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),A::Time=>Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000),A::Bool=>Value::Bool(v&2==0),A::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),A::Json{..}=>Value::Null,A::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),A::Image{..}|A::File{..}=>Value::Null,_=>return None,
    })}
    args.iter().map(|(n,k)|one(k,variant).map(|v|(n.clone(),v))).collect()
}

/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
pub fn operations(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
    assets::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    let fixtures=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    let has_fixture=fixtures.values().any(|r|!r.is_empty());
    if fixtures.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{campaign.unsupported.insert("operation fixture matrix exceeds 1000 rows; provide a focused security fixture".into());return;}
    let mut viewers=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rows) in &fixtures{for row in rows{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!viewers.contains(p){viewers.push(p.clone());}}}}
    for p in &bound.programs{
        if !matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.program.native{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: trusted/system/opaque operation",p.program.name));continue;}
        let target=bound.schema.visible_tables().find(|(t,_)|super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t)).or_else(||bound.schema.visible_tables().next()).map(|(t,_)|t.clone());
        let Some(target)=target else{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no table population for generated arguments",p.program.name));continue;};
        let dummy=Invariant{table:target,target:"read".into(),column:None,predicate:"allow".into(),site:crate::Site::default(),policy:Some(RuleExpr::Allow),operation:Some(p.program.name.clone()),requirements:Vec::new(),syntax:None,unsupported:None};
        let cases=histories::fixture_cases(&p.program.args,&fixtures,&viewers,campaign);
        for (variant,mut args) in cases.into_iter().enumerate(){
            let rows=if has_fixture{fixtures.clone()}else{match snapshot(bound,&dummy,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));break;}}};
            if !has_fixture{for (arg,kind) in &p.program.args {if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind&&let Some(row)=rows.get(table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())&&let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(arg.clone(),id.clone());}}}
            for viewer in &viewers{
                let mut engine=match if has_fixture {fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.matrix",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))}{Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));continue;}};
                campaign.attempted+=1;*campaign.operations.entry(p.program.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
                if p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query{
                    match engine.query(&p.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(_)=>{},Err(e) if e.code=="E_INPUT"=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated arguments rejected as E_INPUT",p.program.name));},Err(_)=>campaign.refused+=1,}
                }else{
                    match engine.mutate(&p.program.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>campaign.refused+=1,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: matrix engine error {e}",p.program.name));},_=>{},}
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn fork(_bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
    if !probes.is_empty(){return Err("fixture probes must be authored operations".into());}
    fixture.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}

snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/engine/postgres/mod.rs:458:    if schema.tables.values().any(|t| t.not_before.is_some()) {
//! Typed server-clock facts used only by the proof abstraction, never runtime rules.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#102-temporal-intent — lifetime is independent of physical sweep lag.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{Value, ir::BinaryOp as Op, schema::{Table, ColumnType}};
// Quarry identifiers cannot contain ':'. No authored field aliases this atom.
const CLOCK: &str = "$security:server-clock";
pub(super) fn clock() -> RuleTerm { RuleTerm::Field(CLOCK.into()) }
pub(super) fn has_clock(rule: &RuleExpr) -> bool {
    rule.any_term(&|t| matches!(t, RuleTerm::Field(f) if f == CLOCK))
}
pub(super) fn comparison(op: Op, a: RuleTerm, b: RuleTerm) -> RuleExpr {
    match op {
        Op::Gt => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lt,b,a), Op::Gte => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lte,b,a),
        Op::Eq => RuleExpr::Eq(a,b), Op::Ne => RuleExpr::Ne(a,b),
        _ => RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b),
    }
}
pub(super) fn admission(table: &Table) -> RuleExpr {
    let mut facts = vec![table.rules.read.clone()];
    for (column,op) in table.not_before.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Lte)).chain(table.expire.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Gt))) {
        let field=RuleTerm::Field(column.clone());
        let edge=comparison(op,field.clone(),clock());
        let edge=if table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(ColumnType::is_optional) {
            RuleExpr::Or(vec![RuleExpr::Eq(field,RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),edge])
        } else {edge};
        facts.push(edge);
    }
    RuleExpr::And(facts)
}

pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, info: &super::super::catalog::TableInfo, expr: &ast::Expr) -> Option<RuleExpr> {
    use ast::{Expr,Literal,TypeKind};
    fn term(info:&super::super::catalog::TableInfo,e:&Expr)->Option<RuleTerm>{match e {
        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now" => Some(clock()),
        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..}) && info.columns.get(name).is_some_and(|t|t.kind==TypeKind::Time) => Some(RuleTerm::Field(name.clone())),
        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Number(n),..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Int(n.parse().ok()?))),
        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Null,..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),
        _=>None,
    }}
    if let Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=expr {
        if op=="and"||op=="or" {
            let xs=vec![bind(catalog,info,left)?,bind(catalog,info,right)?];
            return Some(if op=="and"{RuleExpr::And(xs)}else{RuleExpr::Or(xs)});
        }
        let op=match op.as_str(){"="=>Op::Eq,"!="=>Op::Ne,"<"=>Op::Lt,"<="=>Op::Lte,">"=>Op::Gt,">="=>Op::Gte,_=>return None};
        if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(term(info,left),term(info,right)){return Some(comparison(op,a,b));}
    }
    if let Expr::Unary{op,value,..}=expr && op=="not" {return Some(RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(bind(catalog,info,value)?)));}
    let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:expr.clone(),site:expr.site().clone()};
    super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule).ok()
}
# LLP 3000.036.004: Security invariants and exposure

**Type:** RFC
**Status:** Accepted (SEC design revisions 3/5 and SEC2 amendment/implementation round 5: Muse and fresh Codex READY; qualification in progress)
**Systems:** Snapback 4 Quarry authorization, check, audit, generated security tests, validation
**Author:** Codex for Charlie Cheever (SEC)
**Date:** 2026-10-18
**Revised:** 2026-10-19
**Related:** [LLP 3000.036](./3000.036-what-the-app-farm-asks-of-the-rule-language.rfc.md), [LLP 3000.035](./3000.035-quarry-semantics-in-lean.plan.md), [LLP 3000.014](./3000.014-a-child-reads-through-its-parent.rfc.md), [LLP 3000.038](./3000.038-file-assets.rfc.md)

## 1. Contract

The wave-16 bakeoff found rules enforced correctly but policies encoded wrongly.
A declaration of intent is independent of the rules implementing it. `check`
reports each declaration as **proved**, **refuted**, or **unproved**. Refuted
means a concrete, replayed database/rule counterexample, never merely a Boolean
assignment. Unproved names the unsupported path or incomplete search. Neither
unproved nor a finite passing test campaign may be described as proved.

Implementer: SEC in this worktree, starting 2026-10-18. The author commissioned
this semantic extension and two independent design reviews. No version bump
or publication is part of this lane. STAGE owns provisioning/password dev doors;
RULES5 owns live-parent rule semantics; FRONT owns general diagnostic triage.

## 2. Surface

Use the existing rule vocabulary rather than invent a second policy language:

```
invariant notes.read only .patient = viewer or .clinician = viewer
invariant requests.update only .requester != viewer
invariant members.role write only exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
invariant photos always .image != null
```

The first target is a table, the second component an effect (`read`, `insert`,
`update`, `delete`) or a column followed by `write`. `write` checks insert and
update, including both images for ordinary updates; delete authority has its
own declaration. A column invariant only concerns writes to that column, but
must include every effective admission path. Owner-only field patches bypass
an ordinary table update rule; invariants affected by `written by` stay
unproved until that bypass is modeled. An implication from a denied table
rule must never prove such an owned-field claim.
`always` is a row-integrity assertion. Predicates parse as ordinary Quarry
expressions; unsupported policy expressions are reported unproved with the
reason. Unknown tables/columns/effects are authoring errors. Multiple source
files and multiple declarations are supported. For update intent `.field` means both old and next images for mutable
columns; named transitions require an explicit reviewed reduction or stay
unproved. Once-judged immutable/id rules need a proved equal-image reduction
or stay unproved. Both upsert arms and `insert if absent` are included, with
their own collision/refusal observations. `always` quantifies over every
committed row image from insert/update/upsert and trusted seed/import/restore/
activation, not merely mutation inserts. Constraint-based row proof must use
the actual validation on every ingress, including defaults and the exact
non-whitespace minimum function, or stay unproved.

No declaration changes runtime
authority or replaces a rule. No implicit service-seat exemption is added.

The proof fragment uses bound rule predicates. A row assertion can be proved
from its required column type/constraints (not an optional type), or otherwise
remains unproved. Aggregate/transition/temporal assertions outside that fragment
remain explicitly unproved, not syntactically discarded. Syntax with `now`,
counts, or other program expressions must retain that reason. This is a safe
extension point, not a claim of a complete solver for arbitrary Quarry.

## 3. Sound proof boundary

For a table effect, prove `admission => policy` for **every database snapshot**,
row image, ordinary authenticated viewer (including service seats) and clock.
The engine system principal is explicitly outside this quantification: it is
the trusted seed/import/job boundary, never an ordinary user or an implicit
service-seat exemption. Tables touched by system/native/job/worker paths remain
unproved absent a reviewed reduction, and audit lists those writers. This overapproximates every reachable history:
an old assignment and a changed current role are independent facts. Grant
creation never proves a current role. Required role predicates must be true at
the access boundary. No fixture-specific assumption enters a proof.

Translate Boolean connectives compositionally, retaining true/false/unknown
truth for atomic predicates. Unknown never admits and `not unknown` is unknown.
Atomic facts are keyed by their full bound structure, including table, index,
keys, named equalities and old/next image. Symmetric equality may canonicalize by operand reordering only, preserving
Value variants (Id is not Text), table, index, keys, image and predicate kind;
other equalities and probes stay independent. A finite exhaustive implication
check over this abstraction is sound because it includes every concrete
valuation, even impossible ones. Cap atoms/work; cap exhaustion is unproved.
Do not add database consistency axioms without a separate soundness argument.
Never drop, assume true/false, or simplify an unsupported admission atom;
keep it opaque with its full identity or report unproved. Concrete leaf
semantics are the current `evaluate_leaf`/`term`, not stale Lean open points:
null equals null, null differs from a non-null literal, Id differs from Text,
noninteger ordered comparison returns false, and a missing live reference
marks unknown (including under negation). Missing ordinary fields currently
load null; missing reference and missing image are not conflated. Any semantic
mismatch with the independent model is named, never normalized away. Runtime
errors do not establish an admitted result; probe/storage/schema failure
cannot be used as a successful counterexample or a proof fact.

The Lean addition proves a three-valued Kleene kernel with admission only
on Readable and the exact theorem: exhaustive checking implies that for every
valuation, eval(admission) = Readable implies eval(policy) = Readable; the Quarry-to-kernel translation and runtime boundary remain reviewed
Rust obligations, not a claim of whole-platform formal verification.

For read intent, admission includes the ordinary read rule and every bypass:
- point/list/view/filter/count/exists and live refetch use ruled reads;
- sync acquisition and publication authorize at their own server snapshot. An
  offline device cannot know a later server revocation, even with until-revoked.
  A synced read invariant therefore reports the device access path unproved
  for current-server-role intent, separately from its server proof. Delivered
  history has an additional explicit retained-history blocker. No stale local
  role is treated as a current server role;
- inherited read-through rules are checked in their bound form, including
  captures; live-parent semantics remain RULES5's responsibility;
- valued write targets already reread through `get` before returning, and
  generated insert results also use an ordinary ruled read. Verify those
  reductions against the bound IR; any escaping image without that reduction
  is a blocker or must satisfy the implication `effective write admission => read intent` for that image;
  otherwise the declaration stays unproved;
- maintained aggregates/columns and row views may expose source changes under
  different rules: report such source claims unproved unless a reviewed exact
  reduction proves them; projections narrow payload only; existence/count disclosure follows the
  derived path's own rules, so each derived path needs its own reduction or
  stays unproved;
- table-rule probes see raw target rows, unlike program `exists`. Follow
  reverse dependencies into every other table's read/write rules: the presence
  of a marker row or a successful write can disclose the protected target's
  existence or named fields without returning that target. Source privacy
  intent remains unproved for such raw-probe observations unless a reviewed
  reduction proves authorization or the policy explicitly permits disclosure;
- incoming `on delete cascade` and `on delete set null` effects bypass child
  delete/update rules. Report child delete/update/column-write invariants
  unproved when affected, including transitive cascades, until initiating
  authority and child-image policy are modeled. Enumerate these edges in audit;
- asset reads inherit their owning row privacy, while upload/write authority
  belongs to insert/update intent; applicable private-field authorities must be conjoined with owning-row
  admission for asset reads;
- jobs, schedules, native jobs, actions and external effects are trusted system
  or worker boundaries. Read claims affected by opaque outputs/side effects
  remain unproved. Job writes do not prove ordinary-user write authority;
- refusal codes are observations: an authored difference between an absent
  lookup and later denial is a reported exposure. A privacy proof must not
  ignore that path. Unmodeled constraint/refusal observations remain unproved.

Observer boundary: this verifies row payload and logical existence/refusal
observations, not physical timing, runtime cost or all resource side channels.
The platform's documented examined-count/meter-cut observations are explicitly
excluded and reported as exclusions, not full noninterference. Read intent
includes all logical exposures above. A component may be proved while the
whole declaration remains unproved because another path is blocked.

The implementation must build a closed-world census of bound IR read/write
forms and schema-triggered paths. An unclassified form is a blocker, never
silently omitted. Census includes sequence allocation/read admission, online
and device query paths, ephemeral paths, owner fields, maintained writes and
referential actions. System/job/action/native/external touched tables default
unproved without a reviewed reduction, and are listed as grant-column writers.
Every `read` declaration covers all enumerated logical paths, not just its
direct rule. Authenticated viewer classes use engine operations; signed-out
cases run only against HTTP/auth admission, never a fabricated principal.
Retained-history observations are classified by leave policy and reported
separately; they cannot refute the platform's retention contract or prove a
current-role declaration. Test output names unsupported device freshness.

The report enumerates the paths checked and blockers. A proof is never awarded
just because the direct table rule passed while an indirect path is unmodeled.
Conservative rejection/notes are acceptable; false proof is not.

## 4. Counterexamples and generated tests

Generate deterministic challenges from schema types, policy atoms and fixture
rows. Use in-memory stores; never leave SQLite sidecars under target. Vary the
viewer across owner/other-user/other-role/revoked-role/unauthenticated classes,
policy literals, principal arguments and absent optional values. Existing role
rows and retained grant rows form revoked-role snapshots. Do not call an
arbitrary consistent snapshot a *reachable* application-operation sequence.

Concrete counterexamples must replay the engine's actual rule admission and
show the policy false, recording viewer, effect, row, supporting rows and rule
path. The absence of a concrete witness when implication fails is **unproved**.
Generated tests are a separate executable check, run by default `test`, with

//! The same fixture loader serves query testing and residency at either CLI entry point.
use super::Options;
use crate::project::Project;
use snapback4_core::{Engine, wire};
use snapback4_lang::Bound;

pub(super) fn engine(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    engine_at(project, bound, options, snapback4_core::now_ms())
}

pub(super) fn engine_at(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options, now: i64) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture") else {
        if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            return Engine::new_at(store, bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
        }
        return project.open_engine(bound).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open the store: {error}"));
    };
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut schema = bound.schema.clone();
    // Residency defers ordinary maintains until after bulk loading and builds
    // them once; column derives stay installed and maintain as rows insert.
    let residency = options.flags.contains_key("residency");
    if residency { schema.maintains.retain(|m| m.private.is_some()); }
    let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut engine = Engine::new_at(store, schema, bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut names = Vec::new();
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(fixture).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {fixture}: {error}"))? {
        let entry = entry.map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {fixture}: {error}"))?;
        names.push(entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string());
    }
    names.sort();
    // One batch for the whole directory: the engine orders parents before
    // children within a batch, so file order (and read_dir order) cannot make
    // a read-through child or a reference miss its parent.
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    let mut unknown = Vec::new();
    for name in names {
        let Some(table) = name.strip_suffix(".jsonl") else { continue };
        if name == super::test_cases::FILE { continue; }
        let path = std::path::Path::new(fixture).join(&name);
        let Some(definition) = bound.schema.tables.get(table) else {
            if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") {
                eprintln!("test: skipped {} — saved source declares no table {table}; pass --fixture <dir> to select contemporary fixtures explicitly", path.display());
                continue;
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — the starter's own unmodified sample is skipped loudly; a project's file for a missing table still refuses.
            if !options.flags.contains_key("source") && super::init::starter_fixture(&name).is_some_and(|starter| std::fs::read_to_string(&path).is_ok_and(|text| text == starter)) {
                eprintln!("test: WARNING: skipped {} — the todo starter's unmodified sample rows for a `{table}` table your snapback/*.q does not declare; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's other leftovers", path.display());
                continue;
            }
            unknown.push(name);
            continue;
        };
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture file {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
        for (line_number, line) in text.lines().enumerate().filter(|(_, line)| !line.trim().is_empty()) {
            let at = format!("{name}:{}", line_number + 1);
            let mut json: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(line).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}"))?;
            // A residency fixture is often an exported store: engine-maintained
            // columns are dropped and rebuilt from the source rows. `test`
            // refuses them as an insert and `/seed` do (E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN).
            if residency && let Some(fields) = json.as_object_mut() { fields.retain(|field, _| !definition.maintained.contains_key(field)); }
            relative_times(definition, &mut json, now).map_err(|why| format!("{at}: {why}"))?;
            if let Some(why) = wire::bare_principal(definition, &json) { return Err(format!("{at}: {why}")); }
            let row = wire::row_from_json(definition, &json).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}\n  hint: a fixture row is one JSON object per line with the table's fields and an \"id\"; see snapback4 guide testing"))?;
            rows.push((table.to_owned(), row));
        }
    }
    // A misspelled file name would otherwise load nothing and still pass.
    if !unknown.is_empty() {
        let tables: Vec<&str> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
        // 0.4.17's init wrote the todo starter's rows beside a project's own schema.
        let starter = unknown.iter().any(|name| name == "todos.jsonl")
            && std::fs::read_to_string(std::path::Path::new(fixture).join("todos.jsonl")).is_ok_and(|text| text.contains("\"id\":\"todo-milk\""));
        let starter = if starter { format!("\n  hint: todos.jsonl is the todo starter's sample that `snapback4 init` wrote; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's {} while they are unmodified (or delete them, and the starter's list/add/toggle/remove lines, yourself)", super::test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() };
        return Err(format!("{fixture}: {} name{} no table in snapback/*.q; nothing was run{starter}\n  hint: a fixture file is <table>.jsonl for a declared table ({}), plus {}; see snapback4 guide testing",
            unknown.join(", "), if unknown.len() == 1 { "s" } else { "" }, tables.join(", "), super::test_cases::FILE));
    }
    // Fixture rows take `/seed`'s insert path (defaults, sequences, unique with the author's codes, caps, maintained views), not the trusted bulk import.
    let loaded = engine.seed_at(rows, now).map_err(|error| {
        let text = error.to_string();
        let hint = if text.contains("NOT_FOUND") { "\n  hint: every fixture file loads as one batch, parents first; NOT_FOUND means a referenced or read-through parent row is in no fixture file (check the id)" } else { "" };
        format!("{fixture}: {text}{hint}")
    })?;
    if residency {
        engine.adopt_at(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot build fixture maintains: {error}"))?;
    }
    if !options.json { println!("test: loaded {loaded} rows from {fixture} in {:.1} s", started.elapsed().as_secs_f64()); }
    Ok(engine)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#5-fixtures-and-dev-evidence — relative time leaves, seeds and operations share one captured clock.
/// Only typed time leaves interpret `now`: text and JSON retain their literals.
pub(crate) fn relative_times(table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table, row: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(fields) = row.as_object_mut() {
        for (name, value) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(name) { time_value(kind, value, now).map_err(|why| format!("{name}: {why}"))?; }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn time_value(kind: &snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType, value: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) if !value.is_null() => time_value(inner, value, now),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, serde_json::Value::Array(items)) => {
            for item in items { time_value(of, item, now)?; }
            Ok(())
        }
        (ColumnType::Time, value) => {
            let Some(text) = value.as_str().filter(|text| text.starts_with("now")) else { return Ok(()) };
            let delta = if text == "now" { 0 } else {
                let offset = text.strip_prefix("now").unwrap();
                if !offset.starts_with(['+', '-']) { return Err(format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?}: expected now or now±duration (ms, s, m, h, d, w)")); }
                crate::dev_clock::offset(offset).map_err(|why| format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?}: {}", why.message))?
            };
            let resolved = now.checked_add(delta).filter(|time| (0..=snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX).contains(time))
                .ok_or_else(|| format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?} is outside Unix milliseconds 0..9007199254740991"))?;
            *value = resolved.into();
            Ok(())
        }
        _ => Ok(()),
    }
}
pub(super) fn argument_times(kinds: &std::collections::BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::ir::ArgType>, args: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(fields) = args.as_object_mut() {
        for (name, value) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = kinds.get(name).and_then(snapback4_core::schema::arg_as_column) {
                time_value(&kind, value, now).map_err(|why| format!("{name}: {why}"))?;
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod column_tests {
    use super::*;
    use snapback4_core::{Context, Value};
    #[test]
    fn residency_fixture_import_rebuilds_poisoned_columns() {
        let source=include_str!("../../../snapback4-lang/tests/fixtures/maintained-columns.q");
        let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty());
        let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("f1a-fixture-{}",std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        {
            let files=[("posts",r#"{"id":"p","at":1,"likeCount":999,"selected":999,"total":999}"#),
                ("likes",r#"{"id":"l","postId":"p","active":true,"amount":7}"#)];
            for index in [0,1] {std::fs::write(root.join(format!("{}.jsonl",files[index].0)),files[index].1).unwrap();}
            let options=Options {positionals:vec![],json:true,flags:[("fixture".into(),root.to_str().unwrap().into()),("residency".into(),String::new())].into()};
            let project=Project {root:root.clone()};
            let mut engine=engine(&project,&bound,&options).unwrap();
            let row=engine.query("postRow",Context::new("alice",[("id".into(),Value::Id("p".into()))].into()).at(1000)).unwrap().data;
            assert_eq!(["likeCount","selected","total"].map(|field|row.field(field).unwrap().as_int().unwrap()),[1,1,7]);
            assert_eq!(engine.store().rows("posts").unwrap(),vec![row]);
            for m in &engine.schema().maintains {
                let rows=engine.store().rows(&m.target).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(rows.len(),1);
                assert_eq!(rows[0].field("value").unwrap(),&Value::Int(if m.private.as_ref().unwrap().column=="total" {7} else {1}));
            }
            for (table,_) in files {std::fs::remove_file(root.join(format!("{table}.jsonl"))).unwrap();}
        }
        std::fs::remove_dir(&root).unwrap();
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod order_tests {
    use super::*;
    // `items` sorts before `lists`: the child's file is read first, yet the
    // directory loads as one batch with parents ordered before children.
    #[test]
    fn read_through_child_file_before_parent_file_loads() {
        let source = "use personas alice, bob\n\ntable lists:\n  owner: principal\n  title: text 1..80\n  immutable owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  cap 50 by owner\n  sync to .owner\n\ntable items:\n  parent: lists\n  text: text 1..200\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  insert <- .parent.owner = viewer\n  cap 50 by parent\n";
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("fixture-order-{}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(root.join("items.jsonl"), "{\"id\":\"00000000000000000000000C01\",\"parent\":\"00000000000000000000000B01\",\"text\":\"wash\"}\n").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(root.join("lists.jsonl"), "{\"id\":\"00000000000000000000000B01\",\"owner\":\"dev:alice\",\"title\":\"Home\"}\n").unwrap();
        let options = Options { positionals: vec![], json: true, flags: [("fixture".into(), root.to_str().unwrap().into())].into() };
        let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
        let engine = engine(&project, &bound, &options);
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        let engine = engine.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(engine.store().rows("items").unwrap().len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(engine.store().rows("lists").unwrap().len(), 1);
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod relative_tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::json;
    #[test]
    fn relative_time_is_typed_checked_and_does_not_rewrite_text_or_json() {
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "table notes:\n  at: time\n  optionalAt: time ?\n  title: text <=80\n  payload: json <=200\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let mut table = bound.schema.tables["notes"].clone();
        use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
        table.columns.insert("times".into(), ColumnType::List { of: Box::new(ColumnType::Optional(Box::new(ColumnType::Time))), max: 4 });
        let now = 1_000_000_000;
        for (text, expected) in [("now", now), ("now-1h", now-3_600_000), ("now+2d", now+172_800_000), ("now+1ms", now+1)] {
            let mut row = json!({"at":text, "optionalAt":text, "title":"now-1h", "payload":{"time":"now"}});
            relative_times(&table, &mut row, now).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(row, json!({"at":expected,"optionalAt":expected,"title":"now-1h","payload":{"time":"now"}}));
        }
        for text in ["nowsoon", "now+0h", "now+1.5h", "now-9999999999999999999h", "now-100w"] {
            let error = relative_times(&table, &mut json!({"at":text}), now).unwrap_err();
            assert!(error.contains("E_FIXTURE_TIME") && error.contains(text), "{error}");
        }
        let mut null = json!({"at":42,"optionalAt":null});
        relative_times(&table, &mut null, now).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(null, json!({"at":42,"optionalAt":null}));
        let mut list = json!({"times":["now", null, "now-1s"]});
        relative_times(&table, &mut list, now).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(list, json!({"times":[now, null, now-1000]}));
    }
    #[test]
    fn seed_defaults_and_relative_rows_share_the_captured_clock() {
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "table notes:\n  at: time default now\n  ends: time\n  expire at .ends\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nquery one():\n  return notes[\"n\"]\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("rel3-relative-{}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(root.join("notes.jsonl"), r#"{"id":"n","ends":"now+1ms"}"#).unwrap();
        let options = Options { positionals: vec![], json: true, flags: [("fixture".into(),root.to_str().unwrap().into())].into() };
        let now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
        let mut engine = engine_at(&Project { root:root.clone() }, &bound, &options, now).unwrap();
        let row = engine.query("one", snapback4_core::Context::new("dev:alice", Default::default()).at(now)).unwrap().data;
        assert_eq!(row.field("at").unwrap(), &snapback4_core::Value::Int(now));
        assert_eq!(row.field("ends").unwrap(), &snapback4_core::Value::Int(now+1));
        let gone = engine.query("one", snapback4_core::Context::new("dev:alice", Default::default()).at(now+1)).unwrap().data;
        assert_eq!(gone, snapback4_core::Value::Null);
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }
}

//! The same fixture loader serves query testing and residency at either CLI entry point.
use super::Options;
use crate::project::Project;
use snapback4_core::{Engine, wire};
use snapback4_lang::Bound;

pub(super) fn engine(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    engine_at(project, bound, options, snapback4_core::now_ms())
}

pub(super) fn engine_at(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options, now: i64) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture") else {
        if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            return Engine::new_at(store, bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
        }
        return project.open_engine(bound).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open the store: {error}"));
    };
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut schema = bound.schema.clone();
    // Residency defers ordinary maintains until after bulk loading and builds
    // them once; column derives stay installed and maintain as rows insert.
    let residency = options.flags.contains_key("residency");
    if residency { schema.maintains.retain(|m| m.private.is_some()); }
    let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut engine = Engine::new_at(store, schema, bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut names = Vec::new();
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(fixture).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {fixture}: {error}"))? {
        let entry = entry.map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {fixture}: {error}"))?;
        names.push(entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string());
    }
    names.sort();
    // One batch for the whole directory: the engine orders parents before
    // children within a batch, so file order (and read_dir order) cannot make
    // a read-through child or a reference miss its parent.
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    let mut unknown = Vec::new();
    for name in names {
        let Some(table) = name.strip_suffix(".jsonl") else { continue };
        if name == super::test_cases::FILE { continue; }
        let path = std::path::Path::new(fixture).join(&name);
        let Some(definition) = bound.schema.tables.get(table) else {
            if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") {
                eprintln!("test: skipped {} — saved source declares no table {table}; pass --fixture <dir> to select contemporary fixtures explicitly", path.display());
                continue;
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — the starter's own unmodified sample is skipped loudly; a project's file for a missing table still refuses.
            if !options.flags.contains_key("source") && super::init::starter_fixture(&name).is_some_and(|starter| std::fs::read_to_string(&path).is_ok_and(|text| text == starter)) {
                eprintln!("test: WARNING: skipped {} — the todo starter's unmodified sample rows for a `{table}` table your snapback/*.q does not declare; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's other leftovers", path.display());
                continue;
            }
            unknown.push(name);
            continue;
        };
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture file {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
        for (line_number, line) in text.lines().enumerate().filter(|(_, line)| !line.trim().is_empty()) {
            let at = format!("{name}:{}", line_number + 1);
            let mut json: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(line).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}"))?;
            // A residency fixture is often an exported store: engine-maintained
            // columns are dropped and rebuilt from the source rows. `test`
            // refuses them as an insert and `/seed` do (E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN).
            if residency && let Some(fields) = json.as_object_mut() { fields.retain(|field, _| !definition.maintained.contains_key(field)); }
            relative_times(definition, &mut json, now).map_err(|why| format!("{at}: {why}"))?;
            if let Some(why) = wire::bare_principal(definition, &json) { return Err(format!("{at}: {why}")); }
            let row = wire::row_from_json(definition, &json).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}\n  hint: a fixture row is one JSON object per line with the table's fields and an \"id\"; see snapback4 guide testing"))?;
            rows.push((table.to_owned(), row));
        }
    }
    // A misspelled file name would otherwise load nothing and still pass.
    if !unknown.is_empty() {
        let tables: Vec<&str> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
        // 0.4.17's init wrote the todo starter's rows beside a project's own schema.
        let starter = unknown.iter().any(|name| name == "todos.jsonl")
            && std::fs::read_to_string(std::path::Path::new(fixture).join("todos.jsonl")).is_ok_and(|text| text.contains("\"id\":\"todo-milk\""));
        let starter = if starter { format!("\n  hint: todos.jsonl is the todo starter's sample that `snapback4 init` wrote; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's {} while they are unmodified (or delete them, and the starter's list/add/toggle/remove lines, yourself)", super::test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() };
        return Err(format!("{fixture}: {} name{} no table in snapback/*.q; nothing was run{starter}\n  hint: a fixture file is <table>.jsonl for a declared table ({}), plus {}; see snapback4 guide testing",
            unknown.join(", "), if unknown.len() == 1 { "s" } else { "" }, tables.join(", "), super::test_cases::FILE));
    }
    // Fixture rows take `/seed`'s insert path (defaults, sequences, unique with the author's codes, caps, maintained views), not the trusted bulk import.
    let loaded = engine.seed_at(rows, now).map_err(|error| {
        let text = error.to_string();
        let hint = if text.contains("NOT_FOUND") { "\n  hint: every fixture file loads as one batch, parents first; NOT_FOUND means a referenced or read-through parent row is in no fixture file (check the id)" } else { "" };
        format!("{fixture}: {text}{hint}")
    })?;
    if residency {
        engine.adopt_at(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot build fixture maintains: {error}"))?;
    }
    if !options.json { println!("test: loaded {loaded} rows from {fixture} in {:.1} s", started.elapsed().as_secs_f64()); }
    Ok(engine)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#5-fixtures-and-dev-evidence — relative time leaves, seeds and operations share one captured clock.
/// Only typed time leaves interpret `now`: text and JSON retain their literals.
pub(crate) fn relative_times(table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table, row: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(fields) = row.as_object_mut() {
        for (name, value) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(name) { time_value(kind, value, now).map_err(|why| format!("{name}: {why}"))?; }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn time_value(kind: &snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType, value: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) if !value.is_null() => time_value(inner, value, now),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, serde_json::Value::Array(items)) => {
            for item in items { time_value(of, item, now)?; }
            Ok(())
        }
        (ColumnType::Time, value) => {
            let Some(text) = value.as_str().filter(|text| text.starts_with("now")) else { return Ok(()) };
            let delta = if text == "now" { 0 } else {
                let offset = text.strip_prefix("now").unwrap();
                if !offset.starts_with(['+', '-']) { return Err(format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?}: expected now or now±duration (ms, s, m, h, d, w)")); }
                crate::dev_clock::offset(offset).map_err(|why| format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?}: {}", why.message))?
            };
            let resolved = now.checked_add(delta).filter(|time| (0..=snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX).contains(time))
                .ok_or_else(|| format!("E_FIXTURE_TIME: {text:?} is outside Unix milliseconds 0..9007199254740991"))?;
            *value = resolved.into();
            Ok(())
        }
        _ => Ok(()),
    }
}
pub(super) fn argument_times(kinds: &std::collections::BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::ir::ArgType>, args: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(fields) = args.as_object_mut() {
        for (name, value) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = kinds.get(name).and_then(snapback4_core::schema::arg_as_column) {
                time_value(&kind, value, now).map_err(|why| format!("{name}: {why}"))?;
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod column_tests {
    use super::*;
    use snapback4_core::{Context, Value};
    #[test]
    fn residency_fixture_import_rebuilds_poisoned_columns() {
        let source=include_str!("../../../snapback4-lang/tests/fixtures/maintained-columns.q");
        let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty());
        let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("f1a-fixture-{}",std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();
        {
            let files=[("posts",r#"{"id":"p","at":1,"likeCount":999,"selected":999,"total":999}"#),
                ("likes",r#"{"id":"l","postId":"p","active":true,"amount":7}"#)];
            for index in [0,1] {std::fs::write(root.join(format!("{}.jsonl",files[index].0)),files[index].1).unwrap();}
            let options=Options {positionals:vec![],json:true,flags:[("fixture".into(),root.to_str().unwrap().into()),("residency".into(),String::new())].into()};
            let project=Project {root:root.clone()};
            let mut engine=engine(&project,&bound,&options).unwrap();
            let row=engine.query("postRow",Context::new("alice",[("id".into(),Value::Id("p".into()))].into()).at(1000)).unwrap().data;
            assert_eq!(["likeCount","selected","total"].map(|field|row.field(field).unwrap().as_int().unwrap()),[1,1,7]);
            assert_eq!(engine.store().rows("posts").unwrap(),vec![row]);
            for m in &engine.schema().maintains {
                let rows=engine.store().rows(&m.target).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(rows.len(),1);
                assert_eq!(rows[0].field("value").unwrap(),&Value::Int(if m.private.as_ref().unwrap().column=="total" {7} else {1}));
            }
            for (table,_) in files {std::fs::remove_file(root.join(format!("{table}.jsonl"))).unwrap();}
        }
        std::fs::remove_dir(&root).unwrap();
    }
}
