        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("fixture-order-{}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&root).unwrap();

#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
pub struct Engine {
    timeline_schedule: timeline::Schedule,
    ephemeral: std::sync::Arc<ephemeral::Hub>,
    store: Store,
    schema: Schema,
    programs: BTreeMap<String, Program>,
    ceilings: Ceilings,
    entropy: u64,
    minter: crate::ids::Mint,
    generation: u64,
    /// Per-table physical expiry scan positions. Restricted or retained roots
    /// consume a visit, so later due keys make progress across short sweeps.
    expiry_after: BTreeMap<String, Vec<Value>>,
    /// Dev engines may consult replay policy; release engines never suppress effects.
    dev_effects: bool,
    /// The app's native module, once installed (LLP 3000.005).
    #[cfg(feature = "native")]
    native: Option<std::sync::Arc<native::Module>>,
    /// Lines native jobs logged, for the host to print.
    #[cfg(feature = "native")]
    native_logs: native::Logs,
    /// The server's registry of open connections, given to every context
    /// that does not bring its own; none in tests and `run`.
    presence: Option<std::sync::Arc<dyn Presence>>,
}

#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
impl Engine {
    pub fn new(store: Store, schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_at(store, schema, programs, crate::now_ms())
    }

    /// Activate using the owning host's clock for parent liveness and captures.
    pub fn new_at(mut store: Store, mut schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>, now: i64) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        columns::validate_before_prepare(&schema)?;
        schema.check_row_view_audiences()?;
        schema.prepare_contribution_indexes();
        schema.prepare_delivery_lookup();
        let issues = crate::schema::validate(&schema);
        if let Some(issue) = issues.first() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{}: {}", issue.path, issue.message)));
        }
        let programs = programs.into_iter().map(|p| (p.name.clone(), p)).collect();
        let (generation, metadata) = Self::activate(&mut store, &schema, &programs, None, now, true, None, None)?;
        let dev_effects = metadata.dev_effects;
        store.exclude_ephemeral(&schema);
        Ok(Self { timeline_schedule: timeline::Schedule::default(), ephemeral: std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(metadata.store_id, generation, &schema)), store, schema, programs, generation, expiry_after: BTreeMap::new(), dev_effects, ceilings: Ceilings::default(), entropy: crate::ids::seed(), minter: crate::ids::Mint::default(), #[cfg(feature = "native")] native: None, #[cfg(feature = "native")] native_logs: native::Logs::default(), presence: None })
    }

    /// A detached read owner for HTTP query/sync computation. Creating it is
    /// brief; materializing its response does not hold the serving engine.
    pub fn reader(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Ok(Self { timeline_schedule: timeline::Schedule::default(), ephemeral: self.ephemeral.clone(), store: self.store.reader()?, schema: self.schema.clone(), programs: self.programs.clone(),
            ceilings: self.ceilings, entropy: 0, minter: self.minter.clone(), generation: self.generation, expiry_after: BTreeMap::new(), dev_effects: self.dev_effects,
            #[cfg(feature = "native")] native: self.native.clone(),
            #[cfg(feature = "native")] native_logs: native::Logs::default(), presence: self.presence.clone() })
    }

    /// The registry every program's `connected[p]` reads, on this engine.
    pub fn set_presence(&mut self, presence: Option<std::sync::Arc<dyn Presence>>) {
        self.presence = presence;
    }


    /// Attach to an already activated semantic generation without installing
    /// caller source. Reads of its identity and compiled content are one snapshot.
    pub fn open(store: Store) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::open_at(store, crate::now_ms())
    }

    pub fn open_at(store: Store, now: i64) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::open_selected_at(store, now, None)
    }

    fn open_selected_at(mut store: Store, now: i64, expected_release: Option<u64>) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = store.opening_transaction()?;
        Store::check_selected_release(tx.conn(), expected_release)?;
        let (generation, schema, programs): (i64, String, String) = tx.conn().query_row(
            "SELECT g.generation, g.schema, g.programs FROM _generations g JOIN _meta m ON m.key = 'generation' AND g.generation = CAST(m.value AS INTEGER)",
            [], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?, row.get(2)?)))
            .map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(format!("no readable activated backend in this store: {e}")))?;
        let kept = crate::store::opening::Backend(generation, schema.clone(), programs.clone());
        let generation = u64::try_from(generation).map_err(|_| RuntimeError::Store("negative stored generation".into()))?;
        let schema = serde_json::from_str(&schema).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::decode(e, |e| RuntimeError::Store(format!("invalid stored schema: {e}"))))?;
        crate::schema::timeline::check_version(&schema)?;
        timeline::check_no_rebuilds(&tx, &schema)?;
        schema.check_row_view_audiences()?;
        let programs = serde_json::from_str(&programs).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(format!("invalid stored programs: {e}")))?;
        let mut schema: Schema = schema;
        let stored_schema_hash = schema.hash();
        schema.prepare_contribution_indexes();
        schema.prepare_delivery_lookup();
        let schema_hash = schema.hash();
        // Upgrade derived candidates from older binaries using the stored
        // backend, never a caller's source. The repair logs its row changes.
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9.3 — expiry index adoption is required even without physical cap counters.
        let (generation, metadata) = if stored_schema_hash == schema_hash
            && tx.meta("live_sum_candidates")?.as_deref() == Some("1")
            && (!schema.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none())
                || tx.meta(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META)?.as_deref() == Some(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION))
            && (!crate::rows::caps::required(&schema) || tx.meta("cap_counts_schema")?.as_deref() == Some(&schema_hash))
            && (!schema.tables.values().any(|table| table.not_before.is_some())
                || tx.meta(crate::store::time_audience::PUBLICATION_STATE_KEY)?.as_deref() == Some("1")) { (generation, Store::finish_open(tx)?) }
            else { tx.rollback()?; Self::activate(&mut store, &schema, &programs, None, now, true, expected_release, Some(&kept))? };
        let dev_effects = metadata.dev_effects;
        store.accept_open(); store.exclude_ephemeral(&schema);
        Ok(Self { timeline_schedule: timeline::Schedule::default(), ephemeral: std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(metadata.store_id, generation, &schema)), store, schema, programs, generation, expiry_after: BTreeMap::new(), dev_effects, ceilings: Ceilings::default(), entropy: crate::ids::seed(), minter: crate::ids::Mint::default(), #[cfg(feature = "native")] native: None, #[cfg(feature = "native")] native_logs: native::Logs::default(), presence: None })
    }

    /// Serving a selected immutable release may attach only while that release
    /// is still current. A racing deployment makes startup retry, never adopt.
    pub fn attach_release(store: Store, expected: u64) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::attach_release_at(store, expected, crate::now_ms())
    }

    pub fn attach_release_at(store: Store, expected: u64, now: i64) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        let mut engine = Self::open_selected_at(store, now, Some(expected))?;
        engine.dev_effects = false;
        Ok(engine)
    }

    pub fn memory(schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new(Store::memory()?, schema, programs)
    }

    pub fn schema(&self) -> &Schema {
        &self.schema
    }

    pub fn store(&self) -> &Store {
        &self.store
    }

    pub fn store_mut(&mut self) -> &mut Store {
        &mut self.store
    }

    pub fn program(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Program> {
        self.programs.get(name)
    }

    pub fn programs(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &Program> {
        self.programs.values()
    }
    }).transpose()
}

pub struct Store {
    conn: Connection,
    /// Server stores own timed publication. Device stores receive only rows
    /// the server has already published and never consult this private ledger.
    server: bool,
    // Fields drop in declaration order: retain the sidecar ownership lock until
    // SQLite has closed. No raw descriptor to the database is held here.
    ownership: Option<std::sync::Arc<std::fs::File>>,
    #[cfg(unix)]
    file_identity: Option<(u64, u64)>,
    page_size: u64,
    catalog: BTreeMap<String, i64>,
    /// Where uploaded bytes live: `assets/` beside the database file, or a
    /// directory that dies with the store for a store in memory.
    assets_dir: PathBuf,
    /// A store in memory owns its assets directory: nothing creates it until
    /// the first upload, and dropping the store removes it. Before this, every
    /// `Store::memory()` created one and nothing removed it — one empty
    /// directory per in-memory engine, four million of them in a week of test
    /// runs on one machine, until listing the temp directory took minutes.
    ephemeral_assets: bool,
    identity: activation::Identity,
    #[cfg(test)]
    full_adoption: bool,
    pub adoption_report: adoption_report::AdoptionReport,
}

impl Drop for Store {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if self.ephemeral_assets {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.assets_dir);
        }
    }
}

/// One transaction over the store, with the catalog in reach.
pub struct Tx<'a> {
    activation_scan: Option<activation_scan::Buffer>,
    pub changes: std::cell::RefCell<crate::revocation::Recorder>,
    sequence_table: std::cell::Cell<Option<bool>>,
    tx: rusqlite::Transaction<'a>,
    page_size: u64,
    server: bool,
    catalog: &'a BTreeMap<String, i64>,
}

fn scan_sql(dir: Dir) -> &'static str {
    match dir {
        Dir::Asc => {
            "SELECT r.data, i.k FROM i JOIN r ON r.t = ?1 AND r.id = i.id WHERE i.ti = ?2 AND i.k >= ?3 AND i.k < ?4 ORDER BY i.k ASC LIMIT ?5"
        }
        Dir::Desc => {
            "SELECT r.data, i.k FROM i JOIN r ON r.t = ?1 AND r.id = i.id WHERE i.ti = ?2 AND i.k >= ?3 AND i.k < ?4 ORDER BY i.k DESC LIMIT ?5"
        }
    }
}

impl Store {
    /// Inspect a dev store without creating a database, changing permissions, or
    /// running the writable store's initialization and upgrade path.
    pub fn open_read_only(path: &Path) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        let mut conn = Connection::open_with_flags(path, rusqlite::OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_READ_ONLY)?;
        #[cfg(unix)]
        let file_identity = file_identity(&conn)?;
        conn.busy_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))?;
        let tx = conn.transaction()?;
        let version: String = tx.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'version'", [], |row| {
                row.get(0)
            })?;
        if version != STORE_VERSION && version != dormant::VERSION && version != "snapback4-release-store-2" { return Err(RuntimeError::Store(format!("store version {version} is not {STORE_VERSION}"))); }
        let catalog = tx.prepare("SELECT name, num FROM _catalog")?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
        let identity = activation::identity(&tx)?;
        tx.rollback()?;
        let real = std::fs::canonicalize(path).unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_path_buf());
        let assets_dir = real.parent().unwrap_or(Path::new(".")).join("assets");
        let page_size = conn.query_row("PRAGMA page_size", [], |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0))? as u64;
        Ok(Self { #[cfg(test)] full_adoption: false, adoption_report: Default::default(), conn,
            server: false, ownership: None, #[cfg(unix)] file_identity, page_size, catalog, identity, assets_dir, ephemeral_assets: false })
    }

    /// Classify against one read snapshot of the active generation and rows.
    pub fn successor(&mut self, schema: &Schema) -> Result<Vec<crate::successor::Step>, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.read_transaction()?;
            Self::And(items) | Self::Or(items) => items.iter().for_each(|item| item.fields(out)),
            Self::Not(inner) => inner.fields(out),
            Self::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | Self::Created { key, equalities, .. } => {
                key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_, term)| term)).for_each(|item| term(item, out));
            }
        }
    }

    /// The disjuncts of the rule: an `or` flattens, anything else is itself.
    pub fn disjuncts(&self) -> Vec<&RuleExpr> {
        match self {
            Self::Or(items) => items.iter().flat_map(|item| item.disjuncts()).collect(),
            other => vec![other],
        }
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.6 — service seats read on the server and never enlarge a device's audience.
    pub fn service_grant(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        match self {
            Self::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Id(principal)))
            | Self::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Id(principal)), RuleTerm::Viewer)
                if principal.strip_prefix("service:").is_some_and(|name| !name.is_empty()) => Some(principal),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// Remove only entire service disjuncts; a conjunct, negation or ordinary
    /// principal equality must still pass the ordinary exact-audience check.
    pub fn device_audience_rule(&self) -> std::borrow::Cow<'_, RuleExpr> {
        let terms = self.disjuncts();
        if !terms.iter().any(|term| term.service_grant().is_some()) { return std::borrow::Cow::Borrowed(self); }
        let mut audience: Vec<_> = terms.into_iter().filter(|term| term.service_grant().is_none()).cloned().collect();
        std::borrow::Cow::Owned(match audience.len() {
            0 => Self::Deny,
            1 => audience.pop().expect("one audience"),
            _ => Self::Or(audience),
        })
    }

    /// The device audience of `table_name`'s read rule: service grants
    /// removed, then every same-row disjunct a self-probe absorbs (a members
    /// row read under `.principal = viewer or exists members[.group, viewer]`
    /// is its own witness, so the first disjunct adds no reader). What
    /// remains must still be one of the three invertible forms.
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.000#3.1 — absorption is exact equivalence, never a narrowing.
    pub fn sync_audience_rule(&self, table_name: &str, table: &Table) -> std::borrow::Cow<'_, RuleExpr> {
        let audience = self.device_audience_rule();
        let terms = audience.disjuncts();
        if terms.len() < 2 { return audience; }
        let absorbed = |term: &RuleExpr| terms.iter().any(|other| crate::engine::rules::self_probe_absorbs(table_name, table, term, other));
        // @ref LLP 3000.036.000.000#2.2 — a repeated branch (in either equality order) is one audience.
                out.push(&lane.scan);
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Every table a program writes, by statement kind.
pub fn write_tables(program: &Program) -> Vec<(String, &'static str)> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    stmts_write_tables(&program.body, &mut out);
    out
}

//! Logical observer obligations, separate from the whole declaration verdict.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — pull group authority back to each contributor.
use super::*;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Obligation {
    pub path: String,
    pub status: Status,
    pub reason: String,
    pub repair: Option<String>,
}
impl Obligation {
    fn implication(path: String, admission: Result<RuleExpr, String>, policy: &RuleExpr) -> Self {
        let result = admission.and_then(|a| kernel::implies(&a, policy));
        let (status, reason, repair) = match result {
            Ok(true) => (Status::Proved, "every admitted logical contributor satisfies this policy".into(), None),
            Ok(false) => (Status::Unproved, "observer authority does not imply the source policy".into(),
                Some("retain the privacy fields as bare group keys and apply the source privacy policy to this observer".into())),
            Err(why) => (Status::Unproved, why, Some("use ordinary group keys and a read rule over those keys; keep opaque/temporal derivations private".into())),
        };
        Self { path, status, reason, repair }
    }
}

pub(super) fn obligations(bound: &Bound, inv: &Invariant) -> Vec<Obligation> {
    if inv.target != "read" { return Vec::new(); }
    let Some(policy) = &inv.policy else { return Vec::new(); };
    let table = &bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
    let mut out = vec![Obligation::implication(
        "server point/scan/planned view/count/sum/exists payload".into(),
        Ok(super::columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())), policy)];
    for m in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.source == inv.table) {
        let admission = (|| {
            if m.row_key.is_some() || m.parent_key.is_some() || m.private.is_some()
                || !m.aliases.is_empty() || !m.computed.is_empty() || m.own_keys.is_some()
                || table.read_lifetime_sensitive()
            { return Err(format!("{} -> {}: captured/private/computed/temporal maintenance needs a contributor reduction", m.source, m.target)); }
            let target = bound.schema.table(&m.target).ok_or_else(|| format!("{}: maintained-column observer needs its owning table/field authority", m.target))?;
            if m.by.iter().any(|key|key.contains('.')||key.starts_with('$')||table.recorded.contains_key(key)
                || table.columns.get(key).is_none() || table.columns.get(key)!=target.columns.get(key)) {
                return Err(format!("{} -> {}: group keys are not bare identically typed source/target columns",m.source,m.target));
            }
            if target.through.is_some() || target.read_lifetime_sensitive() {
                return Err(format!("{}: derived observer has inherited/temporal authority", m.target));
            }
            // A target group field equals that field on every contributing
            // source row. Its id/value and all lost fields have no such law.
            pullback(&target.rules.read, &m.by)
        })();
        out.push(Obligation::implication(format!("maintained {:?}: {} -> {}", m.kind, m.source, m.target), admission, policy));
        for next in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|n| n.source == m.target) {
            out.push(Obligation { path: format!("derived chain {} -> {} -> {}", inv.table, m.target, next.target),
                status: Status::Unproved, reason: "downstream contributor substitution is not implemented".into(),
                repair: Some("query the directly protected aggregate, or declare and verify privacy on each derived observer".into()) });
        }
    }
    out
}

fn pullback(rule: &RuleExpr, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleExpr, String> {
    fn term(t: &RuleTerm, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleTerm, String> {
        match t {
            RuleTerm::Viewer | RuleTerm::Literal(_) => Ok(t.clone()),
            RuleTerm::Field(f) if keys.contains(f) => Ok(t.clone()),
            _ => Err("derived rule reads a value/id/lost group field or a reference without a contributor identity".into()),
        }
    }
    Ok(match rule {
        RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Deny | RuleExpr::Public {..} => rule.clone(),
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) => RuleExpr::Eq(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) => RuleExpr::Ne(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b) => RuleExpr::Compare(*op,term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::And(xs) => RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        RuleExpr::Or(xs) => RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        RuleExpr::Not(x) => RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(pullback(x,keys)?)),
        RuleExpr::Exists {table,index,key,provenance,equalities} => RuleExpr::Exists {table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),
            key:key.iter().map(|t|term(t,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?,provenance:*provenance,
            equalities:equalities.iter().map(|(n,t)|Ok((n.clone(),term(t,keys)?))).collect::<Result<_,String>>()?},
        RuleExpr::Created {..} => return Err("created probe has no derived read contributor reduction".into()),
    })
}
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
        let mut reasons=Vec::new();let mut direct=Status::Proved;
        if let Some(why)=&inv.unsupported {reasons.push(why.clone());direct=Status::Unproved;}
        else if inv.target=="always" {
            // This narrow theorem holds on every validated committed image,
            // including seed/adoption, with no assumption about a writer.
            let nonnull=match inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap() {
                RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null))|
                RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null),RuleTerm::Field(f)) =>
                    table.columns.get(f).is_some_and(|t| !t.is_optional() && !matches!(t,snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Json{..}) && !table.engine_field(f)),
                _=>false,
            };
            if !nonnull {direct=Status::Unproved; reasons.push("row assertion is not implied by the implemented required-column theorem; transitions, counts and time require an additional proof".into());}
        } else if inv.operation.is_some() {
            match operation::prove(bound,inv) {Ok(true)=>{},Ok(false)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push("operation admission does not imply the policy; an actual operation challenge is required".into());},Err(e)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(e);}}
        } else {
            for word in effects(inv) {
                let admission=if word=="update" {table.update_rule_judged().into_owned()}else if word=="read" {columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())}else{table.rules.for_effect(effect(word)).clone()};
                let implication=if word=="update"{images::update(table,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())}else{kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())};
                match implication {
                    Ok(true)=>{},Ok(false)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(format!("{word} admission does not imply intent over independent predicates; a concrete replay is required for refutation"));},
                    Err(why)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(format!("{word}: {why}"));},
                }
                // Update implication judges both images. Only id/explicit
                // immutable local fields have the equal-image substitution;
                // owner-field and implicit writers remain separate blockers.
            }
        }
        reasons.extend(blockers(bound,inv));
        let obligations=observers::obligations(bound,inv);
        for o in &obligations {if o.status!=Status::Proved {reasons.push(format!("{}: {}; {}",o.path,o.reason,o.repair.as_deref().unwrap_or("declare an explicit observer policy")));}}
        let paths=exposures.iter().find(|x|x.table==inv.table).map(|x|x.paths.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
        let status=if direct==Status::Proved && reasons.is_empty(){Status::Proved}else{Status::Unproved};
        verdicts.push(Verdict{invariant:inv.clone(),status,direct_rule:direct,paths,reasons,counterexample:None,obligations});
    }
    let mut notes=lints::notes(bound,&exposures);
    let missing=adoption::missing(bound);
    if !missing.is_empty() {notes.push(Diagnostic::new(&Site::default(),"N_SECURITY_INTENT",format!("{} tables have access rules and no security invariants: {}; declare the spec's privacy rules as invariants (`guide security`)",missing.len(),missing.join(", ")),"review snapback/generated/security-invariants.q.draft against the spec; candidates are never auto-accepted").guide("security"));}
    let roles=audit::roles(&exposures);
    let uncovered=adoption::uncovered(bound,&exposures);
    Report{format:1,scope:"logical row/authority intent; all database snapshots; ordinary authenticated viewers including service seats; explicit unproved paths".into(),
        exclusions:vec!["system-principal trust boundary".into(),"physical timing, examined counts and resource/meter side channels".into(),"offline access may use a snapshot predating server revocation".into()],invariants:verdicts,exposures,roles,notes,uncovered,campaign_coverage:BTreeSet::new()}
}
fn blockers(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Vec<String> {
    let schema=&bound.schema;let t=&schema.tables[&inv.table];let mut out=BTreeSet::new();
    if inv.target=="read" && inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,policy)!=Ok(true)) {
        out.insert("wire sequencing/activity metadata has no logical-existence reduction; direct row-rule proof does not establish whole-protocol noninterference".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" {
        if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy {
            let grants=policy.disjuncts().into_iter().filter(|r|!r.any_term(&|term|!matches!(term,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_)))).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
            let global=RuleExpr::Or(grants);
            if !matches!(t.rules.insert,RuleExpr::Deny) && kernel::implies(&t.rules.insert,&global)!=Ok(true) {
                out.insert("insert/unique/upsert collision outcomes can reveal an existing hidden row; old-row read authorization is not proved by new-image insert authority".into());
            }
            if kernel::implies(&t.update_rule_judged(),&policy.old_and_next())!=Ok(true) && !matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny) {
                out.insert("blind update/upsert outcomes can reveal hidden-target existence; read correspondence is unproved".into());
            }
        }
        if !t.written_by.is_empty(){out.insert("owner-field write outcomes can observe targets outside the row read audience".into());}
    }
    if inv.target=="read" && t.columns.iter().any(|(c,k)| matches!(k.required(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Ref{..}) && t.on_delete.get(c).copied().unwrap_or(snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Restrict).acts()) {
        out.insert("parent deletion/referential refusal can observe this child (including default restrict and transitive closures)".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" && t.sync.is_some(){out.insert("device read uses a delivered snapshot; current server role at disconnected access is unproved".into());}
    if t.through.is_some(){out.insert("inherited/captured or live-parent authority requires a separate reduction (RULES5 owns live-parent semantics)".into());}
    if !schema.receipts.is_empty() || !schema.timelines.is_empty(){out.insert("receipt/timeline engine-owned paths require a separate reduction; no affected-table inference is assumed".into());}
    if t.is_expiring() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"delete"|"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("expiry/commit-end retirement can change rows outside ordinary delete rules".into());}
    if t.ephemeral.is_some() || !t.sequences.is_empty(){out.insert("ephemeral/sequence path is outside the current proof reduction".into());}
    if !t.written_by.is_empty() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("owned-field update bypass: table rule alone does not establish field-write authority".into());}
    for (name,other) in &schema.tables {
        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") && other.on_delete.values().any(|a|matches!(a,snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Cascade|snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::SetNull)) && name==&inv.table {
            out.insert("incoming cascade/set-null changes bypass this table's ordinary rules".into());
        }
        if inv.target=="read" {
            for rule in [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete] {
                if kernel::dependencies(rule).contains(&inv.table) {out.insert(format!("raw rule probe/reference from {name} can observe {} independently of its read rule",inv.table));}
            }
        }
    }
    if schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.target==inv.table) && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") {out.insert("aggregate/view target rows are written by engine maintenance independently of their direct table rules".into());}
    if !t.maintained.is_empty() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("engine-maintained columns bypass ordinary application write rules".into());}
    if bound.programs.iter().any(|p|matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action)|p.program.native)
        || !schema.effects.is_empty() || !schema.action_specs.is_empty() {
        out.insert("system/job/action/native/external path requires a reviewed reduction; no affected-table inference is assumed".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" {
        for p in &bound.programs {
            let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && !census::body(&p.program.body) {out.insert(format!("{}: scan/merge/loop or opaque IR path needs a reviewed read-disclosure reduction",p.program.name));}
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(target,_)|target!=&inv.table) {
                out.insert(format!("{} may copy protected data into another table; cross-table disclosure reduction is unproved",p.program.name));
            }
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && audit::authored_refusal(&json) {out.insert(format!("{}: authored refusal observations are not reduced to this read policy",p.program.name));}
        }
    }
    out.into_iter().collect()
}
//! Expanded helpers can write from any expression block.
// @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — legacy eligibility examines the complete expanded write footprint.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn stmts_write_tables(stmts: &[Stmt], out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    for stmt in stmts {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Insert { table, .. } | Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "insert")),
            Stmt::Update { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "update")),
            Stmt::Upsert { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "upsert")),
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, upsert, .. } => out.push((target.table.clone(), if *upsert { "upsert" } else { "update" })),
            Stmt::Delete { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "delete")),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => out.push((target.table.clone(), "delete")),
            _ => {}
        }
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. } | Stmt::Push { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expr_write_tables(value, out),
            Stmt::Emit { payload: value, once, .. } => { expr_write_tables(value, out); if let Some(value) = once { expr_write_tables(value, out); } },
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                stmts_write_tables(then_body, out);
                stmts_write_tables(else_body, out);
            }
            Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
                match domain {
                    LoopDomain::Page(scan) => scan_write_tables(scan, out),
                    LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => { expr_write_tables(start, out); expr_write_tables(end_exclusive, out); }
                    LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, .. } => expr_write_tables(value, out),
                    LoopDomain::Recurse(recurse) => {
                        expr_write_tables(&recurse.seed, out);
                    }
                    LoopDomain::LiteralRange { .. } | LoopDomain::ArgList { .. } => {}
                }
                stmts_write_tables(body, out);
            }
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expr_write_tables(condition, out),
            Stmt::Check { condition, current } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                for key in &current.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { row, .. } | Stmt::Insert { row, .. } => expr_write_tables(row, out),
            Stmt::Update { key, patch, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
            }
            Stmt::Upsert { key, row, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(row, out);
            }
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, patch, condition, .. } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
                if let Some(condition) = condition { stmts_write_tables(&condition.setup, out); expr_write_tables(&condition.value, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Delete { key, .. } => expr_write_tables(key, out),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Break | Stmt::Continue => {}
        }
    }
}

fn scan_write_tables(scan: &Scan, out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    if let Some(filter) = &scan.filter { expr_write_tables(filter, out); }
    if let Some(range) = &scan.range {
        for bound in [&range.gt, &range.gte, &range.lt, &range.lte].into_iter().flatten() { expr_write_tables(bound, out); }
    }
    for key in &scan.prefix {
        match key { IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(expr) | IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(expr) => expr_write_tables(expr, out) }
    }
    for cursor in [&scan.after, &scan.before].into_iter().flatten() { expr_write_tables(cursor, out); }
}

fn expr_write_tables(expr: &Expr, out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    match expr {
        Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Variable(_) | Expr::Input(_) => {}
        Expr::Field { base, .. } => expr_write_tables(base, out),
        Expr::Array(items) => items.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Shape { base, fields } => { expr_write_tables(base, out); fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)); }
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
            expr_write_tables(left, out);
            expr_write_tables(right, out);
        }
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } => expr_write_tables(value, out),
        Expr::Builtin { args, .. } => args.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Get(read) | Expr::Exists(read) => {
            read.key.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out));
        }
        Expr::Block { body, value } => { stmts_write_tables(body, out); expr_write_tables(value, out); }
        Expr::Scan(scan) => scan_write_tables(scan, out),
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            if let Some(after) = merge.position.as_ref().and_then(|position| position.after.as_ref()) { expr_write_tables(after, out); }
            for lane in &merge.lanes {
                if let Some(guard) = &lane.guard { expr_write_tables(guard, out); }
                if let Some(project) = &lane.project { expr_write_tables(project, out); }
                if let Some(value) = &lane.distinct { expr_write_tables(value, out); }
                stmts_write_tables(&lane.setup, out);
                if let Some(source) = &lane.source {
                    if let Some(scan) = source.scan() { scan_write_tables(scan, out); }
                    for value in source.expressions() { expr_write_tables(value, out); }
                }
                scan_write_tables(&lane.scan, out);
            }
        }
    }
}
//! Server clock crossings for `not before` tables.

use super::*;

/// Pin an application writer after it owns the store's writer transaction.
/// Untimed schemas retain their historical clock behavior and metadata.
pub(super) fn pin_writer_time(
    schema: &Schema,
    tx: &crate::store::Tx<'_>,
    requested: i64,
) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
    if schema
        .tables
        .values()
        .any(|table| table.not_before.is_some())
    {
        tx.pin_time_audience_clock(requested)
    } else {
        Ok(requested)
    }
}

/// Observe the durable floor from the same snapshot an operation will read.
/// A different engine can advance between the request prelude and this read
/// transaction; an already-published row must not disappear in that gap.
pub(super) fn observe_reader_time(
    schema: &Schema,
    tx: &crate::store::Tx<'_>,
    requested: i64,
) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
    if schema
        .tables
        .values()
        .any(|table| table.not_before.is_some())
    {
        Ok(requested.max(tx.time_audience_clock()?))
    } else {
        Ok(requested)
    }
}

impl Engine {
    /// Advance the server-authoritative lower-lifetime ledger for one host
    /// request. Detached read engines only observe the durable clock floor.
    pub fn advance_time_audiences_at(&mut self, requested: i64) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
        self.advance_time_audience(requested)
    }

    /// The next private wake deadline. It is host scheduling state and never
    /// appears in a query, sync cursor, change answer, or device database.
    pub fn next_time_audience_at(&mut self) -> Result<Option<i64>, RuntimeError> {
        if self.store.conn().is_readonly("main")? {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let tx = self.store.read_transaction()?;
        let next = tx.next_time_audience()?;
        tx.rollback()?;
        Ok(next)
    }

    pub(crate) fn pin_time_audience(&mut self, requested: i64) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
        if !self
            .schema
            .tables
            .values()
            .any(|table| table.not_before.is_some())
        {
            return Ok(requested);
        }
        if self.store.conn().is_readonly("main")? {
            let floor = self
                .store
                .meta("time_audience_clock")?
                .and_then(|value| value.parse::<i64>().ok())
                .unwrap_or(i64::MIN);
            return Ok(requested.max(floor));
        }
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        let now = tx.pin_time_audience_clock(requested)?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(now)
    }

    /// Pin the request clock and publish at most one due row. One-row commits
    /// are a bounded prefix of the ordered private ledger and have the same
    /// payload/event bound as an ordinary admitted row write.
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.003#10 — an HTTP query materializes only a live dependency it actually encountered.
    pub fn materialize_time_audience_at(&mut self, table: &str, id: &str, requested: i64) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
        self.publish_time_audience(requested, Some((table, id)))
    }

    pub(crate) fn advance_time_audience(&mut self, requested: i64) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
        self.publish_time_audience(requested, None)
    }

    fn publish_time_audience(&mut self, requested: i64, selected: Option<(&str, &str)>) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
        if !self
            .schema
            .tables
            .values()
            .any(|table| table.not_before.is_some())
        {
            return Ok(requested);
        }
        if self.store.conn().is_readonly("main")? {
            let floor = self
                .store
                .meta("time_audience_clock")?
                .and_then(|value| value.parse::<i64>().ok())
                .unwrap_or(i64::MIN);
            return Ok(requested.max(floor));
        }

        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        let now = tx.pin_time_audience_clock(requested)?;
        let opening = match selected { Some((table, id)) => tx.selected_time_audience(table, id, now)?, None => tx.due_time_audience(now)? };
        let Some(opening) = opening else {
            tx.commit()?;
            return Ok(now);
        };

        let row = tx.get(&opening.table, &opening.id)?;
        let current = row.as_ref().filter(|row| {
            self.schema
                .table(&opening.table)
                .is_some_and(|table| table.opens_at(row) == Some(opening.at))
        });
        tx.remove_time_audience(&opening.table, &opening.id)?;

        let Some((_table, row)) = self
            .schema
            .table(&opening.table)
            .zip(current)
            .filter(|(table, row)| table.live(row, now))
        else {
            // Stale/rescheduled/deleted and never-live rows advance only the
            // private ledger and clock, with no public commit or invalidation.
            tx.set_time_audience_published(&opening.table, &opening.id, false)?;
            tx.commit()?;
            return Ok(now);
        };

        let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
        let mut runner = Runner::new(
            &tx,
            &self.schema,
            Context::system().at(now),
            // Structural admission proves one opening fits the ordinary
            // request envelope. Keep runtime accounting bounded as a second
            // check; any failure rolls back the cursor and clock together.
//! Real upload ledgers for foreign-id and non-image placement challenges.
// @ref LLP 3000.038#3-upload — assets retain upload ownership and verified media type.
use super::*;
const PDF:&[u8]=b"%PDF-1.4\n%%EOF\n";
pub(super) fn file(e:&mut Engine,owner:&str,types:&[String])->Result<Value,String>{
    let mime=types.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("application/pdf");
    let (bytes,name)=match mime{"application/pdf"=>(PDF,"proof.pdf"),"text/plain"=>(b"verified text\n".as_slice(),"proof.txt"),"image/png"=>(PNG.as_slice(),"proof.png"),_=>return Err(format!("file ledger needs a verified {mime} fixture"))};
    e.store_file(owner,bytes,Some(mime),name,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn contains(value:&Value,id:&Value)->bool {
    if value==id{return true;}
    match value{Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Map(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Row(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    if initial.values().any(|xs|!xs.is_empty()){
        out.unsupported.insert("foreign-asset replay over populated fixtures needs full ledger transfer; empty-ledger challenges run separately".into());
    }
    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
        let p=&bp.program;
        for (arg,kind) in &p.args{
            if !matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}|ir::ArgType::File{..}){continue;}
            for mode in ["foreign upload","non-image photo"]{
                if mode=="non-image photo"&&!matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}){continue;}
                let mut e=match engine(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),program("$security.asset",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};
                let sample=if mode=="non-image photo"{file(&mut e,VIEWER,&[])}else{match kind{
                    ir::ArgType::Image{..}=>e.store_asset(OTHER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
                    ir::ArgType::File{types,..}=>file(&mut e,OTHER,types),_=>unreachable!(),
                }};
                let sample=match sample{Ok(v)=>v,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};
                let Some(mut args)=arguments(&p.args,0)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge needs remaining argument fixtures",p.name));continue;};
                args.insert(arg.clone(),sample.clone());
                out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
                match e.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(VIEWER,args).at(NOW)){
                    Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>out.refused+=1,
                    Ok(_)=>{if let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)&&rows.values().flatten().any(|row|contains(row,&sample)){
                        out.boundary_failures.push(format!("{} placed {mode} through argument {arg}",p.name));
                    }},
                    Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge engine error {error}",p.name));},
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

            let outcome = MutationOutcome::from_receipt(&outcome)?;
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(outcome);
        }

            return Err(RuntimeError::UnknownOp(format!("{name} is a query, not a mutation")));
        }
        if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action {
            return Err(RuntimeError::UnknownOp(format!("{name} is an action: call it with client.act")));
        }
        if program.kind == ProgramKind::Job {
            return Err(RuntimeError::UnknownOp(format!("{name} is a job: a schedule fires it, or `snapback4 run {name}` does; no caller can")));
        }

            return Ok(MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Failed { why: error.refusal(), current: None }, metered: Metered::default(), tables: Vec::new(), events: 0, replayed: false });
        }

            context.presence = self.presence.clone();

        // reads the facts the server established (`clip.duration`), so
        // they are put in place before it runs. An id that is not the
        // writer's own upload stays as sent and the write refuses it.
        for (name, kind) in &program.args {
            let inner = match kind {
                crate::ir::ArgType::Optional(inner) => inner.as_ref(),
                other => other,
            };
            if !matches!(inner, crate::ir::ArgType::Image { .. } | crate::ir::ArgType::Video { .. } | crate::ir::ArgType::File { .. }) {
                continue;
            }
            let Some(id) = asset_id(context.args.get(name)).map(str::to_owned) else { continue;
            };
            if let Some(asset) = tx.asset(&id)?
                && (asset.owner == viewer || context.is_system())
            {
                // @ref LLP 3000.038#3 — a file argument never reads an image's facts, nor an image argument a file's.
                if (asset.kind == AssetKind::File) != matches!(inner, crate::ir::ArgType::File { .. }) {
                    let wanted = if matches!(inner, crate::ir::ArgType::File { .. }) { "a file" } else { "an image or video" };
                    let error = RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TYPE", message: format!("{name} takes {wanted} and {id} is {}; upload it as {wanted}", asset.kind.word()) };
                    return Ok(MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Failed { why: error.refusal(), current: None }, metered: Metered::default(), tables: Vec::new(), events: 0, replayed: false });
                }
                context.args.insert(name.clone(), asset.column_value());
            }
        }
        tx.savepoint("program")?;
        let mut runner = Runner::new(&tx, &self.schema, context, Meter::new(ceilings, Cut::Abort), tx.next_seq()?);
        let ran = runner.run(&program).and_then(|value| {
            runner.charge_output(&value)?;
            Ok(value)
        });
        // Commit-end expiry can cascade or clear rows that an `after insert`
        // witness names. Final obligations therefore inspect the actual
        // provisional commit state, after every retirement action.
        let ran = ran.and_then(|value| runner.retire_expired_births().map(|_| value))
            .and_then(|value| runner.check_final_exists().map(|_| value));
        let metered = runner.meter.used;
        let tables: Vec<String> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
        let events: Vec<Event> = runner.events.take();
        let action_tables = std::mem::take(&mut runner.action_tables);
        let outbox = std::mem::take(&mut runner.outbox);
        let rows_written = runner.rows_written;
        let mut read_tables = runner.read_tables.clone();
        self.entropy = runner.context.entropy;
        drop(runner);
        let outcome = match ran {
            Ok(result) => {
                tx.release("program")?;
                // @ref LLP 3000.019#4 — P7 reports keep at-most-once custody.
                // @ref LLP 3000.022#6 — a covered report keeps only a receipt at the committed head.
                let receipt_only = rows_written == 0 && outbox.is_empty()
                    && self.schema.delivery.iter().any(|binding| binding.mutation == name);
                let seq = tx.next_seq()? - u64::from(receipt_only);
                read_tables.extend(tables.iter().cloned());
                if !receipt_only {
                    tx.record_commit(seq, now, name, &viewer, program.kind.word(), &tables, rows_written)?;
                    partition::append(&tx, seq, &events)?;
                    effects::record(&tx, &outbox, seq, now, self.dev_effects, &self.schema)?;
                }
                MutationOutcome { receipt_only, state: WriteState::Sent { seq, result }, metered, tables, events: partition::disclosed_event_count(&self.schema, &events, &action_tables), replayed: false }
            }
            Err(error) if error.is_refusal() => {
                tx.rollback_to("program")?;
                let current = match &error {
                    RuntimeError::Conflict { current } => {
                        let mut probe = Runner::new(&tx, &self.schema, Context::new(viewer.clone(), BTreeMap::new()).at(now), Meter::new(Ceilings::default(), Cut::Cap), 0);
                        let value = probe.get(current).ok();
                        value
                    }
                    _ => None,
                };
                let why=seat_refusal(&self.schema,&error,&viewer);
                MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Failed { why, current }, metered, tables: Vec::new(), events: 0, replayed: false }
            }
            Err(error) => {
                tx.rollback()?;
                return Err(error);
            }
        };
        if let Some(op) = op {
            let seq = match &outcome.state {
                WriteState::Sent { seq, .. } => *seq,
                WriteState::Failed { .. } => tx.next_seq()? - 1,
            };
            tx.record_applied(&viewer, op, seq, &serde_json::to_string(&outcome.receipt(Some(op))).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?)?;
            tx.bind_applied(&viewer, op, name, &wire_args, now)?;
        }
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(outcome)
    }

    /// Write rows as the system principal: rules are bypassed, validation,
    /// constraints, aggregates and partition events are not. One commit.
    pub fn seed(&mut self, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.seed_at(rows, crate::now_ms())
    }

    /// Development hosts supply their clock so seed commits share operation and stream time.
    pub fn seed_at(&mut self, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>, now: i64) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        let now = self.advance_time_audience(now)?;
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        let now = time_audience::pin_writer_time(&self.schema, &tx, now)?;
        let count = seed_rows(&self.schema, &tx, rows, now)?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(count)
    }

    /// partition events apply. Returns the number of rows removed (0 or 1).
    pub fn seed_delete(&mut self, table: &str, id: &str) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {

    /// A cap grouping by a `set null` reference does not count its null rows
    /// (LLP 3000.039 §2.2), so it proves no population bound.
    pub fn cap_exempts_null(&self, by: &[String]) -> bool {
        by.iter().any(|column| self.on_delete.get(column) == Some(&references::OnDelete::SetNull))
    }

    pub fn immutable_fields(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &str> {
        self.constraints.iter().flat_map(|constraint| match constraint {
            Constraint::Immutable { fields } => fields.iter().map(String::as_str).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            _ => Vec::new(),
        })
    }

    /// Whether `row` is past its `expire` column at `now`: absent to every
    /// reader, pending the sweep. A null time never expires.
    pub fn expired(&self, row: &Value, now: i64) -> bool {
        self.deadline(row).is_some_and(|at| at <= now)
    }

    /// The index the expiry sweep walks: the first leading with the
    /// `expire` column, as the declaration requires.
    pub fn expiry_index(&self) -> Option<(&str, &Index)> {
        let column = self.expiry_column()?;
        self.indexes.iter().find(|(_, index)| {
                index.is_plain() && index.columns().first().map(String::as_str) == Some(column)
            }).map(|(name, index)| (name.as_str(), index))
    }

    /// The column carrying the principal a `sync to T[...]` audience reads.
    pub fn principal_columns(&self) -> Vec<&str> {
        self.columns.iter().filter(|(_, kind)| matches!(kind, ColumnType::Principal)).map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect()
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum LeavePolicy {
    #[default]
    UntilRevoked,
    DeliveredHistory,
}

/// `sync public`, `sync to .column`, or `sync to target[.prefix, ...]`, each
pub(crate) fn seed_rows(schema: &Schema, tx: &crate::store::Tx<'_>, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>, now: i64) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    let bounded=crate::schema::live::has_live(schema);
    let mut bulk_meter=Meter::new(if bounded {Ceilings::job()}else{Ceilings::unbounded()},Cut::Abort);
    let rows:Box<dyn Iterator<Item=(String,Value)> + '_>=if bounded {Box::new(live_bulk::collect(rows,&mut bulk_meter)?.into_iter())}else{Box::new(rows.into_iter())};
    let rows = through_activation::ancestor_order(schema, rows);
    let targets: std::collections::BTreeSet<_> = schema.tables.values().flat_map(|table| table.recorded.values().map(|field| field.table.as_str())).collect();
    let mut imports = BTreeMap::<(String, String), Value>::new();
    let rows: Box<dyn Iterator<Item = (String, Value)> + '_> = if targets.is_empty() { Box::new(rows.into_iter()) } else {
        let rows: Vec<_> = rows.into_iter().collect();
        // A capture sees the imported target's eventual image, including
        // fields retained by patches, defaults and assigned ordinals.
        // Preview every allocation in input order (counters cross tables),
        // then undo the preview; ordinary writes allocate in that same
        // transaction and still own all validation, events and rollback.
        tx.savepoint("seed_sequences")?;
        let mut staging = Runner::new(tx, schema, Context::system().at(now), bulk_meter, 0);
        for (name, row) in &rows {
            if bounded {staging.reserve_bulk_source(name)?;staging.meter.heap(crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(&schema.tables[name]).saturating_mul(4))?;}
            schema.refuse_ephemeral_import(name)?;
            let table = schema.table(name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {name}")))?;
            sequences::refuse_fields(table, name, row)?;
            let id = row.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
            let old = match imports.get(&(name.clone(), id.clone())) { Some(row) => Some(row.clone()), None => tx.get(name, &id)? }.filter(|row| !table.expired(row, now));
            let mut fields = old.as_ref().map(|row| row.as_object().cloned()).transpose()?.unwrap_or_default();
            fields.extend(row.as_object()?.clone());
            if old.is_none() { for (field, default) in &table.defaults { fields.entry(field.clone()).or_insert_with(|| default.value(now, crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL)); } }
            let mut image = Value::Row(fields.into());
            if old.is_none() {
                let (assigned, full) = staging.sequence_fields(name, image.import_input(&ImportInput(())))?;
                if full { return Err(sequences::exhausted()); }
                staging.commit_sequences(name, &assigned)?;
                image = assigned;
            }
            imports.insert((name.clone(), id), image);
        }
        bulk_meter=staging.meter.clone();
        drop(staging);
        tx.rollback_to("seed_sequences")?;
        Box::new(rows.into_iter())
    };
    let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
    let mut runner = Runner::new(tx, schema, Context::system().at(now), bulk_meter, seq);
    runner.import_targets = Some(&imports);
    let mut count = 0;
    for (table, row) in rows {
        if bounded {runner.reserve_bulk_source(&table)?;}
        schema.refuse_ephemeral_import(&table)?;
        timeline::check_import(schema, &table)?;
        runner.upsert(&table, &row.field("id")?.clone(), row.import_input(&ImportInput(())))?;
        count += 1;
    }
    // Seeds preserve ordinary expired images for a later sweep. A source of
    // a row-keyed view is the established exception: an already-expired
    // seeded image must not enter a committed count basis or revive after
    // a clock rollback. Route only those sources through the common retirement
    // path so reference actions and net maintenance still run transactionally.
    // @ref LLP 3000.039#6.5 — seeded expired row-view sources retire with actions; unrelated expired fixtures remain stored.
    let final_exists = runner.satisfied_final_exists()?;
    runner.retire_seeded_view_sources()?;
    // Seeds historically admit fixture rows whose obligations are not
    // meaningful under the system viewer. Retirement may not invalidate an
    // obligation that did hold immediately before its reference actions.
    runner.check_final_exists_witnesses(&final_exists)?;
    let events = runner.events.take();
    let tables: Vec<String> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
    drop(runner);
    if count > 0 {
        tx.record_commit(seq, now, "seed", crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL, "seed", &tables, count)?;
        partition::append(tx, seq, &events)?;
    }
    Ok(count)
    }

// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#8 — durable and ephemeral seats receive the same static clause for every private cause.
#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
pub fn seat_refusal(schema:&Schema,error:&RuntimeError,viewer:&str)->Refusal {
    let mut why=error.refusal();
    if viewer.starts_with("service:") && let RuntimeError::Rule {table,effect}|RuntimeError::AuthorizationBudget {table,effect}=error {
        if let Some(def)=schema.table(table) {why.message.push_str(&format!("; {table} rule clause: {effect} <- {}",def.rules.for_effect(*effect).quarry()));}
    }
    why
}

#[cfg(all(test, feature = "sqlite"))]
mod update_image_tests;
//! Birth and mirroring of maintained fields. Private totals are authoritative.
// @ref LLP 3000.007.001#3.2 — never probe parent authority or resurrect expired rows.
use super::runner::Runner;
#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
use super::write::Origin;
use crate::{error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError}, schema::{Maintain, reservations::{COLUMNS, ASSETS}}, value::Value};
#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
use crate::schema::maintained_id;

pub(crate) fn write_result(table: &crate::schema::Table, mut row: Value) -> Value {
    if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row { fields.retain(|field,_| !table.maintained.contains_key(field)); }
    row
}

/// Preparation replaces contribution indexes, so refuse forbidden dependencies
/// while the caller's resolved declarations are still present.
#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
pub(super) fn validate_before_prepare(schema: &crate::schema::Schema) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    if let Some(issue) = crate::schema::columns::timeline_issues(schema).first() {
        return Err(RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN", message: issue.message.clone() });
    }
    if let Some(issue) = crate::schema::columns::validate(schema).first() {
        let mut message=format!("{}: {}",issue.path,issue.message);
        for m in schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.private.is_some()) { message=message.replace(&m.target,&m.label()); }
        return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(message));
    }
    Ok(())
}


impl Runner<'_> {
    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    pub(super) fn rebuild_imported_columns(&mut self) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> {
        let declarations: Vec<_> = self.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.private.is_some()).cloned().collect();
        for m in declarations {
            self.rebuild_maintain(&m)?;
            self.mirror_column_pass(&m)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007.001#3.2 — devices preserve synchronized column values;
    // only the server reads private totals or performs their mirrored writes.
    #[allow(unused_mut)] // The server fills the private values; the device only charges.
    pub(super) fn birth_columns(&mut self, name: &str, mut row: Value) -> Result<Value,RuntimeError> {
        let price = self.source_price(name, crate::schema::reservations::BIRTH)?;
        self.reservation_deferred |= self.device && price[2] != 0;
        self.charge_price(price)?;
        if self.device { return Ok(row); }
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        {
        let declarations: Vec<_> = self.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.private.as_ref().is_some_and(|o|o.table == name)).cloned().collect();
        if declarations.is_empty() { return Ok(row); }
        let id = row.field("id")?.clone();
        for m in declarations {
            let total = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, &m.target,&maintained_id(&[id.clone()])))?
                .and_then(|r|r.field("value").ok().cloned()).unwrap_or(Value::Int(0));
            if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row { fields.insert(m.private.as_ref().unwrap().column.clone(),total); }
        }
        }
        Ok(row)
    }

    // Without the server feature the interpreter only runs projected device
    // schemas. Those contain no private maintains, so there is nothing to mirror.
    #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
    pub(super) fn mirror_column(&mut self, _m: &Maintain, _key: &[Value], _total: i64) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> { Ok(()) }

    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    pub(super) fn mirror_column(&mut self, m: &Maintain, key: &[Value], total: i64) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> {
        if self.column_rebuild { return Ok(()) }
        let Some(owner) = &m.private else { return Ok(()) };
        self.refuse_timeline_write(&owner.table, crate::schema::Effect::Update)?;
        let Some(Value::Id(id)) = key.first() else { return Ok(()) };
        self.written_tables.insert(owner.table.clone());
        self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined,1)?;
        let table = self.schema.tables[&owner.table].clone();
        let Some(old) = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, &owner.table,id))? else { return Ok(()) };
        if table.expired(&old,self.context.now.unwrap_or(0)) { return Ok(()) }
        if old.field(&owner.column).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Int(0)) == Value::Int(total) { return Ok(()) }
        let mut row = old.clone();
        if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row { fields.insert(owner.column.clone(),Value::Int(total)); }
        let entries = self.tx.put(&owner.table,&table,&row,Some(&old))?;
        self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries,entries)?;
        self.row_changed(&owner.table,Some(&old),Some(&row),Origin::Maintained)
    }

    // All source attempts use the same complete plan, independent of receipts.
    pub(super) fn reserve_columns(&mut self, source: &str) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> {
        // Preserve the write authority/schema refusal for an unknown raw target.
        if self.schema.table(source).is_none() { return Ok(()); }
        let price = self.source_price(source, COLUMNS)?;
        self.reservation_deferred |= self.device && price[3] != 0;
        self.charge_price(price)?;
        self.column_heap = price[4];
        self.column_prepaid = price[3] > 0;
        let assets = self.source_price(source, ASSETS)?;
        if self.device || self.tx.holds_assets() {
            self.charge_price(assets)?;
            self.column_heap = self.column_heap.saturating_add(assets[4]);
        }
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        { self.assets_prepaid = true; }
        Ok(())
    }
}

snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/why/E_CASCADE_BOUND.md:3:E_CASCADE_BOUND means a delete's cascade has no fixed bound that fits its program. `on delete cascade` and `on delete set null` write every referencing row in the deleting transaction, so a reachable delete needs `cap N by <column>` on the referencing table: a product limit on how many rows one target can have. A plain `unique <column>` proves the same bound with a cap of one; a composite unique key does not. A delete then reserves the cascade's whole worst case — every cap along the chain — whether or not those rows exist, so its cost never depends on rows the caller cannot see; `check` refuses a delete whose reservation cannot fit a mutation (or a job chunk). For an unbounded relation: lower the cap if the domain has a smaller limit; keep `on delete restrict` and delete the referencing rows first in a job (`chunk`), then the target; mark the target (`archived: bool`, filtered by readers) and sweep it in a job; or declare `on delete keep` when the rows should outlive the target. guide writes.
